# THE HARD SAYING — Continuity Ledger

> Established, on-page-safe canon. Writers, auditors and the Head Writer check every fact against this file. It contains
> no hidden canon: secrets live in `bible.md`. If a played episode establishes something new, the Head Writer appends it
> under **Episode log** with the scene id and any branch condition. If something here is contradicted by a played episode,
> the episode wins; fix this file and note it in `bible.md`'s revision log.

---

## 1. Fixed canon (series)

### 1.3 Jack's family (Youngstown)
- Home: two-story frame house on Glenwood Avenue near Brownlee Woods, South Side; detached garage with a space heater,
  radio and a beer fridge. Parish: St. Brigid of Kildare. Jack attended Cardinal Mooney High School ('09).
- **Frank (Francis) Costigan**, 51 — GM Lordstown assembly 26 years, UAW Local 1112; laid off since the June 2009 GM
  bankruptcy. Truck: 1999 Chevy Silverado, forest green, rust over the rear wheel wells, ~212,000 miles, bad muffler, left
  blinker out, St. Christopher on the visor.
- **Maria Teresa (Rinaldi) Costigan**, 49 — secretary at St. Brigid's school.
- **Danny (Daniel Francis) Costigan**, 22 (b. Feb 2, 1987) — Jack's older brother. (Where he is: revealed on-page only
  as the story reveals it; see Episode log.)
- **Gina (Regina) Costigan**, 15 (b. Apr 9, 1994) — sophomore at Ursuline High School.
- **Rosie (Rosaria) Costigan**, 7 (b. Oct 7, 2001).
- **Nonna — Carmela Rinaldi**, 84 — Maria's mother; born in San Giovanni in Fiore, Calabria; came over 1951; lives with the
  Costigans; calls Jack "Giacomino." Late husband: Nonno Antonio Rinaldi, Youngstown Sheet & Tube.

### 1.4 Lena's family (New Orleans)
- Home: raised shotgun on N. Dupre Street, Mid-City, near Bayou St. John; took water in 2005; rebuilt. Parish: Our Lady of
  the Holy Rosary (Esplanade Ave.). Family business: Broussard's Seafood (Banks St.). Lena attended the Academy of the
  Sacred Heart.
- **Tony (Antoine) Broussard**, 53 — owns Broussard's Seafood; Saints season-ticket holder.
- **Celeste (Guidry) Broussard**, 50 — registered nurse (currently at Touro Infirmary).
- Siblings: **Luc**, 24 (NOPD officer); **Tess (Thérèse) Fontenot**, 22 (married to Beau; expecting, due Dec 2009);
  **Remy**, 20 (LSU); **Jude and Josie**, 13 (twins).
- **Mémère — Odile Broussard**, 88 (Tony's mother; Breaux Bridge; speaks Cajun French).
- Lena's late grandmother (maternal) — **Lucille Guidry**, "Grandmère" — the Miraculous Medal was hers.

### 1.5 Looks (from the brief — use exactly)
- **Jack:** 6'0", black hair that needs cutting, pale skin, blue eyes, nose broken once in a CYO basketball fight, stubble
  by noon; St. Michael medal (confirmation); scar through his **left** eyebrow (story told differently every time);
  battered paperback in his back pocket; smokes Marlboro Reds when stressed or drinking; "quitting." Wears his brother's
  old Carhartt jacket, flannels, a gray Cardinal Mooney hoodie, one navy Goodwill blazer.
- **Lena:** ~5'5", sun-streaked honey-brown waves, golden skin, warm amber-brown eyes; soft and curvy; grandmother's worn
  gold Miraculous Medal on a thin chain (touches it when she's thinking or lying — per the brief); thrifted sundresses with beat-up brown cowboy boots
  (Justins) or Keds; oatmeal/gray cardigan in the fog; sometimes a black lace mantilla at Mass.
- **Theo:** tall, tanned, artfully messy blond hair, green eyes, expensive clothes worn like they're cheap; grandfather's
  vintage Rolex Submariner.
- **Laney:** petite, copper hair, porcelain skin, freckles she covers, a laugh you can hear across a party.
- **Margot:** white-blonde hair cut blunt at the jaw, very pale, thick black-framed glasses, all black, oxblood Doc Martens.
- **Kevin:** compact and muscular, crew cut, athletic quarter-zips, a big easy smile that never quite reaches his eyes
  (sanctioned phrase — once, ever).
- **Fr. Osei:** medium height, thin, graying beard, deep laugh lines, a slight limp he never explains; rolled sleeves;
  wooden rosary on his belt.
- **Prof. Hale:** tall, silver hair swept back too long, linen jacket with wine on the cuff, a once-beautiful face gone
  ruddy and broken-veined; devastating smile; dog: an old deaf greyhound named Bishop Berkeley.
- **Julian:** medium height, clean-cut, warm brown skin, short dark hair, calm; guitar calluses; cardigans.

### 1.6 Phones and tech (2009)
Jack: Motorola RAZR V3, cracked hinge, T9; secondhand Dell Inspiron. · Lena: LG enV2 (keyboard), full-sentence texts. ·
Theo: iPhone 3GS; MacBook Pro (Snow Leopard). · Laney: pink BlackBerry Curve. · Kevin: BlackBerry Bold. · Margot: Nokia
brick. · Hale: doesn't BCC.

### 1.7 Places
- **CSU Marisol**, Central Coast of California. Classes begin Mon Aug 31, 2009; Labor Day Sept 7; Thanksgiving break
  Nov 25–29; finals Dec 14–18.
- **Delgado Hall** (Anacapa complex, on the Lagoon): 5 floors, co-ed by room. Ground: lobby, front desk, the Fishbowl
  lounge. Basement: laundry. 2nd: 217 Lena & Laney; 204 Rafi. 3rd: 305 Margot. 4th: 412 Jack & Theo; 420 Kevin & Doug;
  401 Priya (RA); 4th-floor lounge with a whiteboard. 5th: roof door "ALARM WILL SOUND" (it won't). East stairwell: best
  cell reception; windows open six inches.
- **Hollis Hall** (1920s Spanish Revival; Philosophy/Classics): Hale's office 314; seminar room 302 (PHIL 9H); section room
  108 (basement).
- **Pacifica Library** ("the Brick"), **the Commons Lawn**, **Ferrer Tower**, **the Grove** (eucalyptus; monarchs in
  winter), **the Lagoon**, **Campus Point**, **the Marine Lab**, **the UCen** (*Marisol Tide* newsroom).
- **Las Olas** (student town west of the Grove): **Seacliff Road** (Greek row: Pi Lambda Chi, Kappa Alpha Delta, Sigma Rho,
  Alpha Tau Kappa; then surf rentals); **the Lighthouse** (last house on Seacliff before the Point; back porch faces the
  Point; gap in the back fence; a bluff path); **Del Playa Lane**; **Shipwreck Cove** (bonfire beach; wooden stairs; old
  schooner ribs).
- **Marisol Point**: headland, ~70-ft bluff, the decommissioned Point Marisol Light (1890s; dark since 1967), a low stone
  wall, a small dirt lot; a white wooden memorial cross at the wall.
- **St. Brendan the Navigator Newman Center** (across Ocean Road, edge of the Grove): 1962 A-frame, ceiling like an
  overturned ship's hull, blue glass, driftwood crucifix, Marian shrine with votive rack and a small brass memorial plate;
  courtyard with an olive tree and a broken sprinkler. Masses: Sat 5:00 p.m.; Sun 9:30, 11:30, 5:00, 9:00 p.m.; weekdays
  12:10 p.m. Confession: Sat 3:30–4:45; Wed 8:00 p.m.
- **Town** (3 mi east on Mission St.): the Anchor (24-hr diner on the harbor wharf; red vinyl, pie case, jukebox); Café
  Bastille (Thursday open mic); the Wharf Rat (dive bar); Sully's (sports bar); Lupe's (taco truck, Seacliff, 10 p.m.–3
  a.m.); Driftwood Books (cats: Boethius, Mrs. Danvers); Giorgio's (pizza); Old Mission Nuestra Señora del Mar; Marisol PD
  on Anacapa Street; Walgreens on Mission Street; the harbor (sea lions).
- Nearest airport: Santa Barbara (SBA), ~55 min south. Amtrak Pacific Surfliner/Coast Starlight on the bluff; a freight
  horn around 2:10 a.m.

### 1.8 Courses (Fall 2009)
- **PHIL 9H "The Examined Life"** — Prof. Hale; honors seminar; 12 freshmen; Mon/Wed 1:00 p.m., Hollis 302. (Jack, Lena.)
- **PHIL 1 "God, Mind and Morals"** — Prof. Hale; lecture ~400; Thursday 4:00 p.m. discussion section in Hollis 108 led by a
  TA (Jack, Lena).

## 2. Episode log (append per episode)

Format:
```
### S1E0X — "Title" (dates)
- On-page canon established: … (scene id; branch condition if any)
- Flags introduced: …
- Choice-dependent facts (what differs by path): …
- Tallies updated: …
- Lena's resolve at end: …
```

### S1E01 — "Lip Service" (Sat Aug 29 – Sun Aug 30, 2009; tag runs to Mon Aug 31, 12:04 a.m.)
33 nodes (27–29 visited per path). Forks: s06f → s07a kitchen / s07b living / s07c front → s08 · s11 → s12a walked /
s12b→s12c cove → s13 · s20 → s20a followed / s20b (hale | stayed) → s21. Shared codas live in no-choice nodes s12c (cove,
1:40 a.m.) and s13b (lounge, 5:20 a.m.).

**On-page canon (all paths unless marked)**
- s01: Drive came I-40/Flagstaff → Barstow → Bakersfield → Hwy 1; gas $2.99 in CA. Frank sings "Youngstown" under his
  breath; AM radio: Kennedy funeral in the Boston rain, Katrina 4th anniversary. Frank: "He was our guy. And he was wrong
  about the babies." Frank's first sight of the Pacific (turnout on Hwy 1). Envelope from St. Brigid's: $340 (Nonna $200,
  Mom $100, Frank $40, "don't tell your mother there's a forty"). Mom's note: books not pizza; call Nonna Sunday after Mass;
  "Nonna says buy a coat." Frank sleeps in the cab (pillow + brown afghan behind the bench seat); SUB pay runs out in
  October. Danny's message: "Tell the Professor don't be a pussy." *The Moviegoer* (Vintage, $3 library-sale sticker,
  margins full) with the Hale postcard (Radcliffe Camera) as bookmark. Frank's knuckle scar from a 1991 line accident (s04 C1).
- s02: Rafi (unnamed here) carries the mini-fridge to 412 ("No stress, boss"). Lena seen across the Commons Lawn: yellow
  sundress, brown cowboy boots, pelican lamp, talks a parking officer out of a ticket. Nonna's lamp from Big Lots;
  cans of Nonna's tomatoes in the fridge.
- s03: Theo's side of 412: 42" TV, espresso machine (card: "Please don't touch the espresso machine. —J. (Mr. Vance's
  office)"), white Frette sheets, *Point Blank* (1967) poster, MacBook Pro, iPhone dock, grandfather's Rolex. Theo
  arrived 10:40; "looked up Youngstown after the housing email." Theo's mother in La Cañada (Station Fire), a life
  coach named Pierre, dogs and "the Hockney." Nonna's olive-wood crucifix (Holy Land pilgrimage 1988) hung over Jack's bed
  (C2: lower, facing the door). Jack's side: three flannels, gray Mooney hoodie, Danny's jacket on the door hook, St.
  Anthony candle (contraband) in a sock, 41 paperbacks in a milk crate.
- s04: Frank's route home: I-40 to Oklahoma City, "a guy in Flagstaff" (cover story; Jack knows). Frank's list: call
  your mother, call Nonna (phone by her bed now), go to Mass, don't go soft, "Write your brother. He reads 'em twice."
  Frank offers then takes back the St. Christopher ("I need him more than you").
- s05: Floor meeting, 4th-floor lounge. Name tag JOHN C. · 412, JOHN crossed out → JACK. Priya: air horn, laminates,
  "Nobody goes to the Point after dark… That's mine." Kevin Tran (stood up: "Kevin Tran. San Jose."): pre-med, only
  child (claimed) / "never been to a party" (claimed lie: has a sister). Theo's two truths: father has an Oscar / been to
  rehab / never been in love — refuses to say which is the lie. Doug P., WoW shirt, "duuude."
- s06: Seacliff Road runs west along the bluff; the Lighthouse is the last house; the Point to its **north** (porch faces
  it). Man at the end of the driveway (unnamed; wire glasses, gray at temples, Carhartt, bike, American Spirit). Theo's
  flask engraved *R.V.* (Macallan 18). Theo programmed himself into Jack's RAZR as "THEO VANCE (IMPORTANT)."
- s07a (kitchen): Rafi, Room 204, tackle-box "vitamins," "I don't do Xanax anymore," abuela; Joey Abruzzo, Staten Island,
  Yankees cap; Kevin "chaperoning Joey… designated adult"; Kevin signed up to lector next week (C1). Theo/Kevin handshake
  runs a second long.
- s07b (living): Laney (white sundress, pearls) dances on the coffee table to BEP; takes a pill with a Jell-O shot
  ("I have a note. It's notarized."); calls Jack "Sad Irish Poem"; "Y'all would hate each other." Guitar guy on the
  stairs (warm brown skin, short dark hair, gray cardigan) plays "Skinny Love" — unnamed.
- s07c (front): Margot on a Volvo hood; Moleskine "FIELD NOTES — 8/29/09"; cloves rationed until the Sept 22 ban; she saw
  Jack's crucifix in the elevator.
- s08: Lena on the porch: blue sundress, oatmeal cardigan worn at the elbows, brown boots, amber eyes, gold Miraculous
  Medal; LG enV2 (opens like a book) buzzes repeatedly, silenced. "Jagoff" (Jack → Lena; she keeps it). Lena's read:
  Rust Belt, parish school, black hair needing a cut, broken nose. Jack's scar is in the **left** eyebrow (stated s08).
- s09: Lena reads the postcard twice, asks "Who's A.H.?" (medal touch). "My grandmother's dead and she still makes me
  wear it." Lena: from New Orleans; "In Louisiana he'd win" (s08).
- s10: Gap in the back fence; bluff path to the Point; Lena: "Don't take it at night… Laney's sorority friend told me"
  (medal touch). Porch light on a motion sensor goes off; the near-kiss; Jack holds still. Laney shouts "MAGDALENA
  BROUSSARD." Lena: "Only my mother, the Holy Ghost, and Laney when she wants something call me Magdalena." Sloane is
  "Laney's future big" (living route: "in the hot tub," i.e., the front kiddie pool).
- s11: Laney sick on the stairs at midnight; Lena holds her hair ("cher"; "That's the vodka leaving, not you"). Lena
  listens to "Skinny Love" with an unreadable look. Sloane Kittredge introduced by Theo (Pi Lam rush chair). Grey Goose.
- s12a (walked): Laney rides piggyback singing "You Belong with Me"; Laney: "She's *taken*… she's got a — "; "Kaylee
  used to — " (no context). Same dorm discovered: 217 / 412. Desk attendant MARCUS. 217 door whiteboard: LANEY ♥ LENA,
  *ora pro nobis*. C2: Lena: "I have a boyfriend."
- s12b/s12c (cove): Shipwreck Cove (stairs with Christmas lights, schooner ribs, driftwood fire). Sloane: "Everybody's
  Catholic eventually." Theo looks north at the Point; asks about hell and "the exchange rate" on forgiveness ("if
  somebody did something — something really bad"). Theo and Sloane leave 1:40; carnitas from Lupe's; 2:10 freight.
- s13: Sock (gray cashmere) on 412's door. Margot in the 4th-floor lounge at 3 a.m.; spells L-I-N-D-Q-V-I-S-T; lends Jack
  her black cardigan (returned folded outside 305 by morning, s14). Margot's diagnosis: "you do a bit… drywall."
  Walked path: Theo's text 2:14 a.m. "Sock protocol… Abandoner. —T."
- s13b: Theo comes in at 5:20 a.m. barefoot, sand, wet jeans, smelling of salt, kelp and jasmine: "Couldn't sleep. Went to
  look at the ocean." (The girl in 412 is gone by nine.) Priya at 7:02: laminated sign, terrible RA-office coffee.
- s14: St. Brendan's courtyard; broken sprinkler; Fr. Osei (Kumasi, Ghana, "by way of a great many hospitals"; former
  doctor; limp, left leg; rubber boots; wooden rosary): "That's a ring. Or a tooth." Mrs. Fitch: lavender cardigan,
  bulletins, lemon bars, "We lost our best soprano last spring." Tyler Brandt (Veritas; Cincinnati, St. X, Miami
  linebacker; "Brother"). Bulletin: Year for Priests; confession Sat 3:30–4:45, Wed 8:00, "or whenever you knock."
- s15: 11:30 Mass, 22nd Sunday OT Year B (Deut 4; Ps 15; Jas 1; Mark 7), old translation. Organ: "Praise to the Lord,
  the Almighty"; Communion "I Am the Bread of Life"; recessional "Lift High the Cross." Peter Haddad lectors (Facebook:
  "future congressman"); Kenzie Rourke cantors; chastity talk "in a few weeks" (Newman FB page, Joey printed it). Osei's
  homily: Kennedy funeral Mass yesterday; "I am very glad I am not his judge… I am less glad that I have the same one."
  Lena in a black lace mantilla, kneels and receives on the tongue; Jack receives in the hand.
- s15b: Diocesan flu memo says bow at the Peace; nobody does; Purell at the doors. Marian shrine plaque: NORA GRACE
  CALLOWAY · 1989–2009 · "LORD, TO WHOM SHALL WE GO?" Lena lights a candle above it. Osei: "Magdalena. You came back." —
  "I told you I would, Father." Lena: preview weekend in April (s16; true, no medal touch).
- s16–s19 duel `lipservice` (see flags). Lena's sources: Mark 7:8, Aristotle NE II, Pascal *cela vous abêtira*; Jack's:
  Isaiah, Augustine (Tractate 7.8 — corrected by Lena), Kierkegaard, Aquinas on scandal, canon 1184. Lena: "My
  grandmother would've said you bury everybody, and you bury them in the good dress." Lena pays Jack's bus fare
  ("You owe me bus fare, Youngstown"). The 11 bus to the harbor; the orange lady.
- s20: The Anchor (red vinyl, pie case, jukebox with one Mariah Carey); Gloria (GLORIA tag, pencil in a gray bun).
  Marionberry pie: Jack rates it 4/10. Hale alone in the back booth with the Sunday *Times* and a Bloody Mary. The
  Aug 30 NYT Magazine (Memorial) on Hale's table; Lena sees it, touches the medal, leaves calmly; leaves a five. Laney:
  Lena warned her she walks out of places "and makes red beans." Laney introduces herself pageant-style.
- s20a (followed): wharf railing, sea lions; Lena: "Allergies. Eucalyptus." (lie, medal touch). "Fine is what we do."
- s20b (hale | stayed): Hale remembers "the nine pages"; "Your Chesterton was good. Your Aquinas was borrowed. Your anger
  was the only original thing in it." Calls him "Mr. Costigan." Pays for Laney's pancakes ("For the cautionary tale").
  Leaves unsteady before 2 p.m. Hale "folds the magazine closed and tucks it under his arm."
- s21: Maria: "I'm seeing your brother next Sunday." Frank called from outside Amarillo. Nonna on the bedroom extension,
  EWTN on, her own rosary (Glorious Mysteries); "E la ragazza?" Gina: "nonna says go to confession. i said for what. she
  said he knows." (all paths)
- s22: Nonna's laundry card: *Whites in HOT. Darks in COLD. MEDALS OFF.* Lena on the east stairwell (between 3 and 2),
  barefoot, nightly call from Wes (8:04 PT): "I didn't read it. Mama said don't." "I love you too."
- s23: Hale's email (no BCC) "Come prepared to prove you have a soul." Lena's address mbroussard@marisol.edu visible to
  all. Two firefighters died in the Station Fire today; Theo's mother at the Peninsula with the dogs; the Hockney's in
  the car. Theo's FB status: "is rooming with a Catholic. Taking bets on who converts whom."
- Music cues (all on the period sheet): s01 Springsteen "Youngstown"; s02 Phoenix "1901"; s03 MGMT "Time to Pretend";
  s07b BEP "I Gotta Feeling"; s08 Grizzly Bear "Two Weeks"; s12a Taylor Swift "You Belong with Me"; s12b Fleet Foxes
  "White Winter Hymnal"; s19 Regina Spektor "Laughing With"; s23 Bon Iver "Re: Stacks". Diegetic: MJ "Rock with You,"
  "Skinny Love," Miley "Party in the U.S.A."

Duel result macros (exclusive and exhaustive; verified by enumeration):
RIGOR `duel.lipservice.rigor>=3` · HEART `duel.lipservice.heart>=3` · WIT `duel.lipservice.wit>=2 &&
duel.lipservice.rigor<=2 && duel.lipservice.heart<=2` · DRAW (new) `duel.lipservice.rigor>=2 && duel.lipservice.rigor<=2
&& duel.lipservice.heart>=2 && duel.lipservice.heart<=2 && duel.lipservice.wit<=1` · LENA `duel.lipservice.rigor<=1 &&
duel.lipservice.heart<=2 && duel.lipservice.wit<=1 || duel.lipservice.rigor<=2 && duel.lipservice.heart<=1 &&
duel.lipservice.wit<=1`. `duel.lipservice.lena` = 3 base, 4–5 if Jack used Reframe (s16) and/or Personal (s17).

**Choice-dependent facts**
- Danny in prison is said aloud to the floor only if `e1_twotruths_danny` (the choice text no longer says "prison").
- Lena's grandmother "died in the storm, in '05" is told to Jack only if HEART (`duel.lipservice.heart>=3`). Bible §14
  had this for E04: E04 must test the macro.
- Lena's "I have a boyfriend" only if `e1_asked_lena_out`; "taken" heard on every walked path; "Wes" on all paths at s22.
- Hale has met Jack and seen his face only if `e1_met_hale` (hale or stayed). Followed path: Hale has not met him.
- The scar: version per listener in the flags above; Fr. Osei saw it was "a ring. Or a tooth" (all paths).
- Laney's surname "Price" heard by Jack at s07b (living) or s20 (all paths, her pageant intro).

**Tallies after S1E01**
- Scar tellings: Theo → `scar_told_theo`; Lena → `scar_told_lena` (+ `scar_told_lena_2` "archbishop" if s20a C2);
  Osei → `scar_told_osei`. Floor (s05 C0) heard "bar fight in Erie" as the lie.

- Lena's resolve at episode end: **6** (s08 → 6, s15 → 8, s20 → 6).
- Duel record: one duel; result per macro (RIGOR = Jack, LENA = Lena, HEART/DRAW = draw, WIT = no decision).
- Anchor pie ratings: marionberry 4/10.

**Notes**

  `cast`; it must never feed a player-facing People list. Journal cards use only names heard on the page.

### S1E02 — "The Examined Life" (Mon Aug 31 – Sat Sep 5, 2009, 12:31 a.m.)
31 nodes. Forks: s12f → s12a box / s12b board → s13 · s17 → s17a laney / s17b margot → s18. Duel `soul` (opponent
Hale, scored as Jack vs. Lena) runs s03–s06v. Written for the after-s1e01 save (followed · walked · living · replyall ·
coughed); several prose callbacks assume those E01 forks (see knowledge.md S1E02 note). s01 C0 is gated on
`e1_email_reply=="replyall"`, s01 C3 on `scar_told_lena_2=="archbishop"`, s08b C2 on `e1_met_laney_early`.

**On-page canon (all paths unless marked)**
- s01 (Mon 3:07 a.m.): Delgado fire alarm (310 residents); goose-horn alarm, fog, eucalyptus. Gina's text: "is the fire
  near u. nonna saw california on the news. she says st florian is the fire one" (Station Fire). Priya: Fremont High
  hoodie, air horn, laminate COVER YOUR COUGH. H1N1…; a second laminate says she has friend-requested the whole 4th floor
  (route for Priya as Jack's FB friend). Kevin co-runs the roll call with a clipboard. Margot's Moleskine "ALARM 1."
  Theo: navy silk robe, rescues the espresso machine. Doug Pruitt exits barefoot by the far **east** fire door, wrong end
  from 420, pillow under his arm (sleepwalker plant; no comment). Laney (sleep mask, pearls, camisole, Lena's oatmeal
  cardigan) hears "COSTIGAN" from Priya: "Like a cop show." Lena: black WHO DAT Saints T-shirt, Keds. Theo/Lena meet;
  Theo: "The roommate. The one he abandoned at the Lighthouse. I dared him anyway." All clear 3:31.
  Hoodie branch: Laney adopts Jack's gray Mooney hoodie (keeps it through E02). Archbishop branch: Lena laughs outright.
- s02 (Mon 12:51–1:12): Hollis 302, oak table, **fourteen chairs** (Hale + 12 freshmen + 1 spare), knocking radiator,
  sea window. PHIL 9H = 12 freshmen: Jack, Lena, Caleb (Visalia; Darwin-fish laptop; *Consolations* "molting" with
  Post-its; read it six times; says "Selah"), Yusuf (Fremont; aerospace; fasting for Ramadan), a Palo Alto girl, a quiet
  chemistry kid, two unrelated San Diego boys with the same surname, and four more Jack doesn't learn till October.
  Jack's navy Goodwill blazer ("a lifeguard at a funeral" — Theo). *Spe Salvi* (Ignatius ed.), bought for $2 off the
  sidewalk cart at **Driftwood Books on Mission, Sunday evening while waiting for the 11 bus**; Benedict holy card as
  bookmark. Jack on Benedict: Regensburg; *Spe Salvi* §2 "The one who has hope lives differently"; 48 sections after.
  Lena on Benedict: "follow him into a burning building… wouldn't put him on my wall"; cites the Jan 2009 Williamson
  excommunication lifting and his written admission (letter to bishops, Mar 2009) that someone could have caught it on
  the internet; "I'd still have somebody open his mail." Hale arrives 1:07, sets down only the roster; "Prove to me you
  have a soul. You have ninety minutes and I've had lunch." Identifies Jack when Jack's chin comes up at "Youngstown";
  names the nine pages and the Reply All; tells the room about the Anchor ("a boy who went after her like a spaniel").
  Lena: "I'd left something on the stove" (thumb to medal). Syllabus wk 1: Descartes *Med.* II; Ryle ch. 1; *ST* I
  qq. 75–76. Hale's whiteboard hand "like barbed wire" (Hale's image; not reused for anyone else).
- s03–s06v duel content: Descartes/Ryle (category mistake, the University); Dennett (Caleb); Yusuf's fasting joke; Lena:
  soul = form of the body. Phineas Gage (Sept 1848, Cavendish VT; bar 3'7", 13¼ lb; "no longer Gage"; Chile; died in his
  mother's house). Lena's hotel argument ("If I *am* my body…"). Hale: "I mark commitment, not variety." Hale's
  inheritance argument (Thebes/Zeus; Riyadh). **Yusuf corrects Hale: from Riyadh you face west; from Fremont nearly due
  north** ("I have a compass"). Lena: genetic fallacy, Lincoln–Douglas at thirteen; "So is the multiplication table."
  Hale: "Your mother could show her working." Caleb: "It's true of *me*. I got out." Hale knows John 6:68 ("I was made to
  learn it") on e2_john668. Whiteboard assignment: TWO PAGES. EVERY WEEK. Prove the thing you least believe you can prove.
  Swap with your appointed enemy. Annotate in RED. Due Wednesdays. Pairs: Palo Alto girl + chemistry kid; Caleb + Yusuf;
  two unnamed pairs; the San Diego boys; **Jack + Lena for the semester**. Hale's dog (levels its bowl with papers).
- s07: Cloister walk; Ferrer Tower carillon (off-key). Caleb learns from Jack that Hale wrote back ("One line. Mostly a
  dare."); Caleb wrote Hale four letters, no reply. Yusuf: iftar invitation (Islamic Center on Mission); father works at
  **NUMMI** (closure announced Thu Aug 27): "Load-bearing wall of the house, that job."
- s08 (Tue 4:10 a.m.): Theo watching *The 400 Blows* (Criterion). Theo: "French cinema… There's a midterm"; **"undeclared
  the way Switzerland is undeclared"** (first said here). 8:40: Passion Pit "Sleepyhead" on the dock; Jack's good flannel
  has a lime Jell-O-shot stain over the pocket (from Saturday, living route). Theo's "corsage" line; swan callback. Gina:
  "mom told nonna ur bringing a nun home 4 thanksgiving. nonna lit a candle" (Gina at Ursuline). UCen books total
  **$186.40** (PHIL 1 reader, Plato, calculus); St. Brigid's envelope $340 minus shower shoes, extension cord, two packs.
  Theo's father's card = "the Meridian card." Driftwood Books sign: BOETHIUS AND MRS. DANVERS ARE NOT FOR SALE ($2–4).
- s08b: Laney at 8:58 with Lena's red beans in Tupperware; Pi Lambda Chi recruitment Friday. Theo plays Sloane; Jack plays
  "Whitney, Atherton." Laney: 120 girls, 49 bids, "forty point eight three" then **"I'm an Aquarius"** (correct for Feb 14).
  Laney's contact in Jack's RAZR: "LANEY (FIREMAN'S FAVORITE)." Laney's T-shirt: Highland Park Lacrosse (non-hoodie).
- s09 (Tue 9:30): PHIL 1 in Sandoval Hall 1 (400 orange seats, 1971), row M. Lena's tote: OCTAVIA BOOKS · NEW ORLEANS;
  black lace mantilla folded on her books (she does sometimes veil at weekday Mass; bare-headed Thu Sep 3). Hale on the
  Euthyphro. Margot's folder THE COSTIGAN PROBLEM ("And every father" — E01 loves branch); PHIL 1 is her gen-ed; Margot =
  305. Lena and Margot meet (handshake). Mantilla/black-clothes exchange; "internalized a two-thousand-year-old men's
  club." Lena: Aquinas I q.6, neither horn. Margot: "You outsourced him" (Lena has no answer yet). Margot: "useful idiots
  for Christian nationalism… Goldberg, *Kingdom Coming*." **Lena: registered Democrat; her father has voted for Mary
  Landrieu three times; the death penalty makes her ill.** Margot: "useful idiot for two parties." C0: Bell *Holy Anorexia*
  vs. Lena's Bynum *Holy Feast and Holy Fast* (Lena wins that point). `e2_hale_jab`: Hale's "Both of me" from the stage.
- s10 (Tue 6:50): Bus 11 runs down Mission to the harbor. Jack's résumé (Mooney '09, Latin Club, point guard, St. Brigid's
  grounds crew). Anchor boards: PIE OF THE DAY: BOYSENBERRY; **JACK'S RATINGS: MARIONBERRY 4/10**. Gloria hires him as
  dishwasher; Laney ("your redhead friend") told her his name at the Anchor Sunday.
- s10b: **$8/hr, Tue/Thu/Sat 7 p.m.–1 a.m., tip share; Saturdays start Sep 12** (Gloria's nephew Hector home from Fresno
  State this weekend). The Hobart. Rudy Salcedo (40s): rosary tattoo on left wrist, Louis L'Amour *Flint* (taped spine),
  a ballgame on the transistor, calls Jack *mijo*; **did six years at Soledad, washed dishes there**; on e2_rudy_visit his
  mother visited every other Sunday, three buses from Oxnard. Gloria's "lattice-top" verdict on Jack; Gloria married a
  fisherman ("smelled like bait"; qc branch).
- s10c (Wed 1:15 a.m.): Last 11 bus 12:30 a.m. Rafi (204; "had you as fridge guy"; dad's place two blocks up from the
  wharf; abuela) lends Jack **La Flaca**, a rusted single-speed beach cruiser with a wire basket, coaster brakes, tilting
  seat, "till Christmas." Jack rides 3 miles home; moon three nights short of full. Glenwood Avenue childhood hill.
- s11 (Wed 6:15): 4th-floor east stairwell landing has the one cell bar. Rosie's drawing (Priority Mail, postmarked Mon;
  Hannah Montana in a modest bikini; JACKS CHURCH). Frank home safe; "Good grip. Saturday."; "How's the pelican?";
  "Costigans don't get the easy ones." **Traficant released Wed Sep 2 (Rochester, MN); Valley welcome party $20.** Frank:
  "A crook. But he was our crook." Maria: chicken; told the school office he's home for Thanksgiving; "the veil girl."
  Nonna: "Giacomino. Primo venerdì… You go *and* you go" (First Friday Mass + confession). Gina: "nonna thinks california
  is in mexico." Jack tells Frank about the job only on `e2_dad_call=="told"`.
- s11b: Letter to Danny (signed "—the Professor"; "There's a girl from New Orleans… argues like a prosecutor"; "Don't. I
  know."); P.S. "Dad doesn't know about the dishes" on hid. Letter carried in Danny's Carhartt; mailed Thu at noon.
- s12 (Wed 8:02): Newman House lounge: 1994 plaid couch, dead ping-pong net, *Da Vinci Code* with Fitch's Post-it "Read
  CRITICALLY." Osei's rubber boots. **Becca Lindell**: Coeur d'Alene, homeschooled, 7 siblings, ranch, butchers deer;
  running the **Newman Welcome BBQ & Beach Cleanup, Sat Sep 19**; roommate = nursing major from Spokane. Joey (wants to be
  a priest). Becca asks if Jack has a girlfriend "for the seating chart." `e2_becca`: yes / days / maybe.
- s12f: Confessions Wed 8:00 in the converted coat closet ("KNOCK (gently)"). Osei's question: "The box, or the board?"
- s12a (box): Jack: "My nonna sent me." Closet confessional: kneeler + chair, linen screen. Osei: "God is not a customs
  officer"; penance a decade + call your grandmother; Italian sisters taught Osei arithmetic in Kumasi. Full absolution
  formula. Skunk on the bike path. **Jack confesses Wed Sep 2.**
- s12b (board): Osei's office: highlife (E.T. Mensah & the Tempos; his father's record), stethoscope on a hook, a framed
  photo **face down in an open drawer** (Osei closes it with his knee on the limp branch), chess set with chipped white
  queen's bishop. Osei on Benedict: "A shy man made to stand on a balcony." Pierogi. Danny's jacket: "He's not using it
  right now." Osei's funeral proverb (the man laughing loudest at the funeral is carrying the coffin) — this is the
  season's one indirect diagnosis of Jack's joking (tic 17); no one else may say it aloud in S1. Twi "Ɛyɛ" (yeah branch).
- s13 (Thu Sep 3, 12:10): St. Gregory the Great, memorial, white; Col 1:9–14 ("inheritance of the holy ones"), Lk 5:1–11;
  Osei's homily (Peter → a job; Gregory begged off; "God also usually says no"). **No Creed** (weekday memorial). ~15 at
  Mass: Mrs. Fitch (lemon bars), old man with cane, Becca, Kevin (Bible saves Jack a seat). Lena bare-headed, Greek
  grammar; Greek at 1:00 (Kevin's lab partner is in it). Anacapa Commons: Kevin's salad; mother **Mẹ** on the BlackBerry;
  Kevin tells her he studied ("Gen chem. AP credit — she thinks it covers orgo"); sister **Tina, UOP dental school,
  4.0**. Kevin: Lena's relationship status is on Facebook, "Doug checked." Theo/Kevin: "Tran." "Vance." Kevin: "My
  parents came on a boat. I don't get to have a bad semester" (family branch). Kevin: "Met him at the floor meeting"
  (theo branch).
- s14 (Thu 4:00): PHIL 1 section, Hollis 108 (basement, ankle-height window, 18 desks). **TA Evan Marsh**: lanky, gray at
  the temples, ~30 (says 29), wire glasses, tan Carhartt, bike helmet, yellow American Spirits; accent from somewhere up
  near Canada; "office hours: when I'm not finishing." Jack recognizes him as the man at the end of the Lighthouse
  driveway. Section: Kierkegaard, will one thing; Marisol Surf hoodie girl; Sigma Rho visor kid. Lena: "Love would be a
  climate I have." Jack: "Everything I've done since I was sixteen…" C0: Evan "doesn't go to parties… twenty-nine";
  asks who Jack went with ("My roommate dragged me"; no name).
- s15 (Thu 4:58): Papers swapped Wed in seminar. Jack's paper "That Faith Is Not an Inheritance" (St. Brigid's baptism;
  Pascal *cela vous abêtira*; "I stayed because I tried the door"); Lena's red: NO / *Cite me.* / "This sentence is lying."
  Lena's paper "That the Body Is Not a Rental" ("Hotels don't bleed"; injury objection deferred to next week); Jack's red:
  "best paragraph I've read this year. I hate it." / Coward. / Okay, fine. Lena on the stairs-overheard call: tells Jack
  **Wesley Arceneaux, Tulane civil engineering, "build the walls right"; at sixteen took his uncle's boat out four days in
  the storm pulling people off roofs.**
- s15b: The Reliquary: on "lit" Lena takes the 2nd cigarette and shows the 1st (Saturday's) from the cardigan pocket;
  **"I'm going to need a container" plays on every branch** (tin in E03). Laney's ironing for Friday.
- s16 (Fri 1:52 a.m.): La Flaca chained to the 4th-floor radiator pipe. Jack friend-requested Lena Tue; she accepted
  1:37 a.m. Wed. Her FB: "Magdalena Broussard · In a Relationship with Wesley Arceneaux · Marisol · Classics '13 · New
  Orleans, LA"; Mardi Gras photo on St. Charles (Wes, #9 Brees jersey, holding the ladder). Laney's wall post (Jell-O
  shots, Wed 2:14 a.m.); Tess Fontenot: "call mama." Wes's status Thu 9:52 p.m.: "is so tired of reading about the Army
  Corps of Engineers." **People You May Know: Nora Calloway (candle avatar), 1 mutual friend Priya Raman**; Jack knows the
  name from the brass plate under the St. Brendan's votives. C0: Nora's page — Selma High School, Marisol; brother Mateo
  ("happy early bday sis. 8 days," Mon 9:40 p.m.); aunt Graciela Gutiérrez (abuelita lights a candle every morning).
  C1: Wes photos (crawfish boil, fleur-de-lis calf tattoo, Jesuit High lacrosse, back row). C2: Jack's wall post "Bus
  fare: $1.25. Interest accrues. —Youngstown" at ~1:57; **Wes Arceneaux likes it within a minute** (no on-page
  inference); Lena replies 2:03: "Interest is usury… Aquinas."
- s17 (Fri 7:12): Theo left at six with unfamiliar keys. Laney's text (FIREMAN!!!…). Margot (peacoat, thermos, brass
  binoculars) recruits Jack + Kevin as "male control subjects" for a *Tide* piece on recruitment; Aug jobs report 9.7%.
  **Kevin keeps his mother's Honda Odyssey on campus in the Anacapa lot** (E11 road trip "borrows" it — same car; no
  conflict). Margot walks: "It's a mile."
- s17a (laney): Moon rising full over the hills, peach. Laney's white strapless with a scorch under a braided belt (left
  hip). Theo's **1979 Mercedes 450 SL, champagne gold, cracked oxblood leather, two seats — his grandfather's**. Laney
  calls him "Theodore" (sanctioned exception: Laney's pageant register; no other character uses it casually in S1 so
  Jack's first angry "Theodore" lands). Sloane on the Pi Lam steps ("The Catholic"). Laney's silver pillbox engraved
  **DRP**; dry-swallows a pill; Jack sees, Lena sees him see. Lena's LG: 8:04, 8:06, 8:09 call to voicemail: "He's
  studying." Lena calls Laney "cher." C1: "I'm not her mother. And her mother's the reason."
- s17b (margot): Bluff bench across Seacliff from Pi Lam (70 ft above the sea). Margot's clove unlit (ban Sep 22). Kevin
  knows the actives from their public FB. Lena walks Laney to the steps, leaves alone, clocks the stakeout. Theo's car
  passes west; Kevin watches it out of sight. Laney on the side steps with white wine and "something out of the clutch"
  (Kevin: "She weighs maybe a hundred and five"). C0: Laney's speech lags; "Don't tell Lena… tell her… Don't."
- s18 (Fri 11:40): Campus Point beach fire: Jack, Lena, Laney, Theo, Margot, Kevin, Doug (Bakersfield Drillers
  blanket); Rafi surfing. Laney: Sloane says she's a "lock." Lena's phone (11:52 text) goes into her boot. **Lena teaches
  the Who Dat chant** ("five Yankees and a Texan"; Saints lost the preseason finale to Miami 10–7 Thu; "Nine days to
  Detroit"). Margot's deadpan chant. Theo films it ("Week one"). Theo's *R.V.* flask (Macallan) goes around; **Theo
  himself does not drink**; Kevin skips it and blocks Laney ("Respectfully, no"), gives her water. Kevin whistles
  perfectly. Margot drinks once.
- s19–s20 (Sat 12:22–12:31 a.m.): Lot atop the bluff, one sodium lamp. Rolex (steel) + champagne SL "bought the same
  week." Cassette adapter. Theo's pitch: Malibu house, pool; Station Fire still burning behind La Cañada; his mother on
  the Peninsula (Palos Verdes). Tonight: 101 south, top down, needle at 85 past Ventura; Jack texts no one. Tomorrow: Theo
  learns of Kevin's Odyssey from Jack. Broke: Theo leaves at nine, gas bought.

  s18 C2 until `e2_apologized` (s15 C3). If he never apologizes, **E03 opens with "Mr. Costigan"** (the thaw belongs to
  E03). Apology nets trust −1 overall (jab −2, apology +1).
- Jack's winter/summer: **on `e2_john668` Jack says that at sixteen, after Hale's book, he tried to put the faith down
  "a whole summer" and stopped going** (summer 2007, before Nov 23, 2007). Canon: Hale's book nearly took his faith that
  summer; the station-bench prayer that November was where he found "nowhere to go." §17 stands; this is the honest
  elision of it. Do not contradict in later episodes.
- Confession: Jack confessed Wed Sep 2 only on `e2_osei=="box"`. **Nonna's First Friday (Mass + confession) and the
  "call your grandmother" penance are left unpaid on both paths** — deliberate: E03 can have Nonna ask.

  machine)**; on used Jack shops at Driftwood.

- E03 entry per `e2_la` (tonight: Jack arrives in Malibu at dawn with Theo; tomorrow/broke: the convoy in the SL and
  Kevin's Odyssey).

- Anchor pie ratings: marionberry 4/10 (now on a board by the register). Boysenberry: unrated.

### S1E03 — "Labor" (Sat Sep 5 – Mon Sep 7, 2009; tag Mon 11:52 p.m.)
30 scenes. Forks: s01b → s02a sl / s02b van → s03 · s09 → s09a getty / s09b pool → s10. Duel `labor` (Lena) runs
s11–s11v. Written for the after-s1e02 save. Prose that assumes E02 forks: **s01 opens on `e2_la=="broke"`** ("You told me
you couldn't afford LA"); the tin assumes `e2_cig_lena!="lit"` (s03 slip No. 1 only; s13 slip "No. 2"; key "second

**On-page canon (all paths unless marked)**

- s02b (van): Journey sing-along; Margot's Durkheim note. Impressions: Laney→Sloane ("philanthropy"), Kevin→Priya ("Per

- s03 (10:04, Pea Soup Andersen's, Buellton): Hap-Pea and Pea-Wee; Theo three bowls; Laney's windmill spoon. **The

- s05 (5:30–8:19): Fridge of chef's containers labeled **R.V. — MON — DO NOT**. Jack drives Lena in the SL to the Point

- s07 (Sun 5:50): Theo's bed empty; **the SL left at 2:40 (Margot)**. Margot hasn't slept; Brideshead (Back Bay ed., $3

- s08 (9:00, Our Lady of Malibu): Irish supply priest; Isaiah 35, James 2 (gold rings), Mark 7 (*Ephphatha*); one-line

  Frank). s11c C1 adds the 2,400-mile drive to see the Pacific; s11d C0 adds "Sunday gravy, a garage you could eat off
  of." s11v "Is he okay?" / s13c "give the whole Pacific a three" fire only when told and not personal (s13c also on
  personal + apology).
- Personal (s11d C4/C5): Lena is "perfectly pleasant to Mr. Costigan" that night; towel at midnight; Monday morning she
  calls him nothing until **s13b C3 apology** ("Apology noted, Mr. Costigan… Costigan. Get in."). Her s15 text is curt
  ("Wednesday, as assigned") on personal; the "thank you for stirring" text needs stirred and (not personal or apologized).
- Richard's hundred: returned (Richard laughs: "First friend he's had I couldn't tip") · kept (folded in *Brideshead*,
  Theo watched; it goes to Danny inside the book unless Jack takes it out) · theo (into Theo's jeans, through the pool) ·
  two ($200; "This one I like"; Theo trust −1).
- Theo knows Danny is in Trumbull on all paths; [plain] five years, a fight, a man hurt badly, pleaded, three more years
  maybe less (Maria's public version); [joke] "a very exclusive school in Ohio"; [keep] Jack asked him to keep it.
  [theo_hi] Theo spoke to Danny ("He's a menace." / "Look out for him").

  double"; whisper "Nonna will remember that" — **owed a payoff in E04–E06**).
- Lena's s15 text: getty "On Wednesday it's Herakles against Gage…" / pool "On Wednesday I bleed on Gage…".
- Poppy knows "the Catholic"; on nun she knows "Sister Jack."

### S1E04 — "Nativity" (Tue Sep 8 – Sat Sep 12, 2009; tag runs to Sun Sep 13, 12:28 a.m.)
28 scenes. Forks: s06b → s07a point / s07b iftar → s08 · s09b → s10 now / s09c stayed → s10s → s10b. No duel (the
yelling-at-God argument is conversation, s10d2). Written for the after-s1e03 save. Gated on E01–E03 vars: `e1_mass_prayer`,
`e2_alarm` (hoodie), `e2_curb` (friend), `scar_told_margot=="nun"`, `e3_tin` (class), `e3_lena_monday` (family),
`e3_told_lena_frank` (and `!e3_duel_personal`), `e3_danny_told`, `e3_nonna`, `e3_getty`, `reliquary`. The two-hander
(s10/s10s–s10f) is ≥4.0k words on every path (4.0k–4.6k).

**On-page canon (all paths unless marked)**
- s01 (Tue 9:41, Sandoval 1, Row M): fog off the Lagoon. Odyssey glass WHO DAT · 6 DAYS. **The Hopkins**: Penguin *Poems
  and Prose* ed. W. H. Gardner, CARDINAL MOONEY H.S. LIBRARY — DISCARD in purple on the page edges, a quarter off the
  discard cart junior spring, came west in the milk crate; it now rides in Jack's back pocket with the Benedict holy card;
  *Brideshead* squared up on the desk for Danny. Margot in Row M; **Lena's aisle seat (on Margot's far side) empty** —
  "both lectures to date." Hale (wine on the cuff, hair wet) declines the clip-on, carries the handheld, never uses it;
  Obama school-speech joke; Hume *Dialogues* X, Epicurus; **"The problem of evil is a bill… Someone always pays it, and it
  is never the theologian."** Jack has had Lena's number since Saturday (Kevin's card). [e4_text] wit sms / plain "u ok" /
  notes (two pages of Hume in Mooney cursive).
- s02 (Tue 12:10, St. Brendan's, Nativity of Mary): Lena sms 11:58 "I’m fine. —L." [wit/plain only]. ~25 people; Mrs.
  Fitch sets **one white rose in a bud vase beside the brass plate** and moves it a quarter inch (plant; untagged). Kevin
  saves Jack a seat with a Bible open to Micah. The organist (still unnamed to Jack) plays; lector stumbles on Ephrathah;
  Osei reads the whole genealogy; homily: Tamar, Rahab, Ruth, "the wife of Uriah" — "God is not ashamed of his family."
  Lena receives kneeling on the bare floor; lights a candle above the plate and the rose; Osei: "Magdalena." Jack: "Happy
  birthday to her." → Lena: "To who?" (medal; lace stops moving) → monsignor smile; "I’ll see you Wednesday. Bring yours."
  [e4_shrine] candle (Jack's $1; Lena's "Thank you") / kneel (Hail Mary; `e1_mass_prayer=="lena"` callback) / joke ("Not
  today, Youngstown," said kindly; sleeve touch).
- s03 (Tue 9:20, dish pit): Vin Scully on the transistor; half-price chowder Tuesdays. **Gloria: Thursday Sep 10 the
  kitchen is dark — hood cleaners, once a year; pie & coffee only.** Jack loses ~$48 + tip share; no windfall (Richard's
  hundred went into the pool, `e3_hundred=="theo"`). Board: LEMON MERINGUE; MARIONBERRY 4/10. Otis "Dock of the Bay";
  **Frank's story: Otis whistled the fade, died three days later in a Wisconsin lake.** [e4_rudy] asked → **Rudy: six years
  at Soledad; his mother every other Sunday, three buses from Oxnard; Louis L’Amour; "Whoever you got — write him."** /
  hobart ("Soledad's was meaner"; fries) / head_down (eggs, rye; "Dish man eats first. Soledad rule.").
- s03b (Wed 12:38, back booth): **Hale drinks alone in the Anchor's back booth some nights** (Gloria: "Professor from up
  the hill"); lemon meringue untouched; "The spaniel washes dishes." Recites **Housman, *A Shropshire Lad* LIV**, first
  stanza, to the window, in a northern voice. **Fixed wording, every branch: "Go home, Mr. Costigan. You’re sober and
  you’re boring me."** A fifty for an $11 check; Gloria: "He tips like he’s apologizing." Cab, Marisol Yellow.
  [e4_hale] housman (Jack finishes the stanza; Mrs. Pagliarulo, Mooney junior English; "darling") / home / water.
- s04 (Wed 1:46, 4th-floor lounge): Priya alone, Fremont High hoodie, Safeway cupcake, one white candle, HAPPY BIRTHDAY
  mylar balloon (its twin tied at the cross). **Nora Calloway (Jack's knowledge, from Priya):** freshman roommate, Delgado
  222; from Selma (raisins; a five-pound bag); philosophy; soprano, Newman choir; sang the Memorare in the shower; moved to
  Serra sophomore year; fell at the Point end of April; **Sept 8 would have been her 20th**; "Accidental" on the form;
  Facebook PYMK because Priya friended the floor. Priya: Hindu, has never been inside St. Brendan's. Jack tells her about
  the plate. [e4_priya] what (argued with **professors** in office hours — no name; fitted sheet; harmony on Happy
  Birthday) / how (the seaward side of the wall, legs over, "where she went to argue"; police closed it in a week) /
  candle (Marlboro lighter; "Happy birthday, Nora"; "not tell anyone about the cupcake").
- s05 (Wed 12:58, Hollis 302): radiator; Caleb's molting *Consolations*; Yusuf fasting. Lena swaps papers: "‘The One Who
  Pays.’ Bold. I’ll be cruel about it by Thursday." **Her page two: "Objection: A statue doesn’t feel the arm go.

- s06 (Wed 5:24): Priya's laminate THE LOUNGE CLOSES AT 2 A.M. **Margot's whiteboard** (doc insert; C 4 · B 1) —

### S1E05 — "Moral Luck" (Sun Sep 13 – Fri Sep 18, 2009; tag Fri 2:16 a.m.)
28 scenes. Forks: s09 → s10a pref / s10b brick → s11 · s13b → s13c stay / s13d walk → s13e. Duel `luck` (s04–s05v, six
scenes). No extended two-hander (per the standing every-other-episode rule; E06 owes one). Ensemble setpiece: Sully's
(s02–s02c) and Bid Day (s13). Written for the after-s1e04 save; gated on earlier vars: `e2_friday`, `e3_told_lena_frank`,
`e3_lena_monday`, `e4_followed` (the Personal option), `e4_seminar`, `e4_walk`, `lena_knows_danny`, `reliquary`. The
Lordstown "Twenty-six years" line in Lena's mouth (s02 C1) assumes the E03 s11c C0 / s11d wording, which is true on this
save. Per-path ≈ 12.1k–13.1k words (mean 12.5k; validator total ~20.4k counts every branch). 22 choices per path; keys 6–12;
whispers 0–5 (6 placed).

  pocket.** No Mooney hoodie (it lives in 217). Odyssey back glass: WHO DAT · TODAY (Lena, 1 a.m., flashlight in her
  teeth; Theo saw her). **The Hopkins stays squared on the desk "till October and a mailer"**; the O'Connor (*A Good Man Is

29 scenes. Forks: s02b → s03a theo / s03b margot → s04 · s11b → s12a sullys / s12b eid → s13. No duel (season record
stands 3–1). **The extended Jack/Lena two-hander is Act Four, s09–s10c** (porch · beach · creek mouth · lifeguard tower ×2 ·
Marine Lab stairs · Commons · Lagoon bench), ≈3.6k–4.1k words per path (mean 3.8k; the blueprint asked ~4,000 — the min
path runs ~400 short; logged, not padded). Per-path episode ≈13.2k–13.9k words (validator total ~19.0k counts every branch);
24 choices per path; keys 6–12; whispers 0–6 (8 placed).

Friendship," s08–s08v). Per path ≈12.7k–13.7k words (validator total ~19.3k counts every branch); 25 choices per path; keys

### S1E08 — "The Little Way" (Tue Sep 29 – Sun Oct 4, 2009; tag Sun 10:55 p.m.)
34 scenes. Forks: s09 → s11a sauce / s11b seals / s11c horses → s11d · s13 → s13b confession / s13c (sleep) · s14 → s15a sullys /
s15b becca → s16 → **s16b (end; new node added in revision)**. Duel **little** ("On the Little Way," s05d–s05v; Lena's feast,
unofficial). Per path ≈12.7k–14.1k words (validator total ~19.3k counts every branch); 24–25 choices per path; keys 6–12;
Lena whispers ≤2 per path (twin choice at s06c); 8 whispers placed. Jack's resolve enters 9, ends **7–10** (engine clamps at 10).
Lena's resolve ends **6**. No dangerous window.
**Save-locked:** written for `saves/after-s1e07.json` only. Ungated lines that assume this save: `e6_hale_danny` (s05 "Hale gave
me that one. For Danny"; s08 the Housman ships; s12b "Has it gone?"), `e7_housman=="sleep"` (s08 letter "a man who doesn't
sleep"), `e7_stairs=="stairs"` (s09 "Last Friday he took those stairs up past her"), `e7_veil=="boyfriend"` (s13 Maria
"She has a *fidanzato*"), `e7_thu=="margot"` (s12 "since the *Tide* office last week"), `e4/e6_goodnight=="magdalena"` (s05b C2
"He has said *Magdalena* to her twice before"), `wes_visit_known` (s02 countdown "Ten days", s05 "Nine days", s15a "5 DAYS"),
`e7_sun=="sullys"` (s14 "Since last Sunday he also owns… gold Mardi Gras beads"), `reliquary==4` (s06b C2 is No. 5),
`e7_doubt=="twoam"` is gated where it appears in prose but **ungated in the s12c Becca journal card** ("She's been praying for
two in the morning"), trait gates (sincerity 50, candor 25, reckless 10, wit 18 — all open on this save). Gate before any other
save reaches E08.

### S1E09 — "Landfall" (Fri Oct 9 – Mon Oct 12, 2009; tag Mon 8:04 p.m.)
31 scenes. Forks: s06 → s06a walk (Seacliff two-hander) / s06b point → s07 · s09b → s10a mass / s10b sullys → s11. Duel **camel**
("On the Rich Young Man," s11b–s11v; the Delgado kitchen, unofficial, no referee). Per path ≈13.7k–14.4k words (validator total
~21.8k counts every branch); 25 choices per path; duel ≈1.13k–1.39k words per path. 6 whispers placed (s04c knee, s04c "Laney, no",
s06a not-looking, s07 fisher-said, s08c "I will", s11b Personal). Jack's resolve enters 10, ends **6–9**. Lena's resolve ends **5**.
No dangerous window. **Save-locked:** written for `saves/after-s1e08.json` (`e8_dance=="waltz"`, `e8_danny=="told"`,
`e8_becca_end=="kiss"`, `e8_relief=="named"`, `e8_reply=="true"`, `e2_margot_side=="lena"`, `e3_roux=="show"`, `e4_wed=="iftar"`;
trait gates reckless≥11, candor≥33, sincerity≥55, wit≥21 — all open on this save). Gate before any other save reaches E09.

- s10a [mass] (9:30, St. Brendan's): 28th Sunday OT (Wis 7:7–11; Heb 4:12–13; Mark 10:17–30). **Damien of Molokai canonized that
  morning (Rome); Osei: "He stayed… *We lepers.*"** Blue-and-gold light. Wes kneels beside Lena. Becca's maple bar (JACK napkin) → Wes;
  "he shares." Mrs. Fitch: "You two… And *you* two!" Tyler fist-bumps Wes ("Brother"). [point] Becca tells the table she prayed for

- s10b [sullys] (10:02): Kevin drives (49ers cap). **Lions–Steelers 20–28 (Dale: "Close."). Browns 6, Bills 3 at Buffalo in wind;
  Cundiff 24 and 18 (0:23 left); Anderson 2-for-17, 23 yds, 1 INT.** Wes adopts the Browns; two Bloody Marys before noon; "like watching
  a levee hold." Becca sms 11:02 (maple bar saved). Lena arrives at halftime from the 9:30: "Y'all are *friends* now." **Frank calls

- s11 (5:40, the kitchen off the Fishbowl): Priya's laminate THIS KITCHEN IS NOT A RESTAURANT. **Gumbo: chicken and andouille (Jacob's,

- s12 (Mon 8:20, Anacapa lot): **Columbus Day: Hale cancelled his own seminar; campus otherwise open; Becca traded for the holiday
  tank shift.** Lena drives Wes to SBA in the Odyssey (Kevin: "Sixty-five. Not sixty-six."). Becca's banana bread for the plane. Wes:
  "Don't let him get away with nothing." **Wes shakes Jack's hand instead of hugging: "See you, Ohio… Remember what I said."** Becca:

- s13 / s13b (8:01–8:04 p.m., 4th-floor landing): Becca sms 8:01 "Did Wes land ok? I prayed for his flight :)" [e9_reply] true / best

### S1E10 — "I Call the Living" (Tue Oct 13 – Sat Oct 17, 2009; tag Sat 6:01 p.m.)
37 scenes. Forks: s13p → s13a hide / s13b run → s14 · s16b → s17a mass (→ s17c) / s17b fishbowl → s18. Duel **cor** ("On the Heart,"
s10–s10v; the Brick, eighth floor and north stairwell, 4:00–4:16 a.m., unofficial, no referee). Per path ≈13.6k–14.3k words
(validator total ~19.9k counts every branch). 8 whispers placed (s04 Priya "never"; s10b Confess; s10b Personal ×2 variants; s15
still; s19 stay; s19 pillbox; s21 Becca "I don't know if I'm —"); Lena ≤2 per path. Jack's resolve enters 8, ends **6–10** (clamped).
Lena's resolve ends **5–6** (6 only on `e10_dawn=="still"`). No dangerous window. **Save-locked:** written for
`saves/after-s1e09.json` (`e9_take=="drink"`, `e9_talk=="saints"`, `e9_point=="walk"`, `e9_waltz=="lena"`, `e9_poem=="knee"`,
`e9_fisher=="wit"`, `e9_wes_danny=="told"`, `e9_tank=="anemone"`, `e9_face=="beach"`, `e9_tin=="quit"`; gates candor≥40, wit≥25,
reckless≥12 — all open). Lena's s10b accusation and Confess are gated on `e9_take=="drink"`; s03 Gloria has a `said` branch.

**On-page canon (all paths unless marked)**
- s01 (Tue 3:20 a.m., Delgado overhang): **Alarm 3, Tue Oct 13, 3:20, in the first storm of the fall; all-clear 3:41.** Margot: 25
  days; "Either he's irregular or he's two people." Doug barefoot with his pillow through the puddle (no comment). Laney in a Hefty bag
  and pearls under Theo's golf umbrella; Theo carried both bags (gray and black). **Lena and Laney not speaking** (since Monday, the
  Madonna Inn tin). Lena in Wes's green Tulane sweatshirt (left behind), "Morning, Jack." Laney: "I've been *exiled*. From my own
  *room*. Over a *tin*." **Laney sleeps on the 412 Aerobed Mon–Thu nights** (in Theo's navy silk; Theo in the black Meridian robe).
- s02 (Tue 3:50 p.m., lobby mailboxes): no mail Monday (Columbus). **Danny's third letter** (Leavittsburg postmark Fri Oct 9): the
  Hopkins came; read "Felix Randal" to Ma; Housman LIV made Reggie cry; Ma told him about Becca; "Did you tell Lena hey or do I got to
  come out there"; "I told him I don't wish. I decided. It's different."; push-ups 140; Browns 0–4 (sic, per Danny). **Second money
  order** 4:35 p.m., post office on Mission: $20.00 ($21.10 with fee) to D. KOSTELNIK, STRUTHERS, OH; FROM blank; stub behind the
  Mooney ID with the first. The day attendant (not Marcus) at the desk.
- s03 (Tue 9:15 p.m., the Anchor): empty in the gale; Gloria plays Sinatra "Summer Wind" on the jukebox. **Dulce (50s) and Rosario
  (~25), Harbor Inn housekeeping,** waiting on a 7 bus; Gloria feeds them free. **Rudy translates: Lena brings soup Sundays to the Harbor
  Inn for the night shift; Dulce says it needs salt.** Jack didn't know (`knows_harbor_soup`). Gloria on Merle per `e9_fisher`. Last 11
  bus 12:40, Dulce and Rosario asleep on it.
- s04 (Wed 1:40–2:20 a.m., basement laundry): storm water in RA STORAGE; **the banker's box CALLOWAY · 222 · HOLD FOR FAMILY** (Serra
  sent it to Delgado in May because 222 was the address on file; Priya signed for it; there since April). Contents: SELMA HIGH
  sweatshirt, CDs, mug, photo of Nora and Priya (bangs) in a dorm window, composition book: "Nora Calloway · Delgado 222 · If found,
  please return. Reward: I'll sing you something." Priya has never read past the cover. **Priya: failing orgo (she tutors it); parents
  run a motel in Fremont; library overnight shift Wed midnight–8.** **Oct 14 is Priya's 21st birthday:** her mother's ("Amma")
  midnight text arrives at the dryers; she doesn't answer it. New laminate: BASEMENT FLOODED · DRYERS 1–3 ONLY. The box now lives in
  401 under the window. The basement piano: dead F, "nobody in Delgado admits to playing."
- s05 (Wed 3:05, 412): Jack's first sentence: *A man knows his own heart by what he says yes to.* Aristotle NE II, James 2:18, Binx,
  Augustine's ten books. Theo awake with a French film.
- s06 (Wed 8:10, 412): Laney plays Jay Sean "Down" for 217 ("an olive branch with a beat"). **Theo and Laney's yellow pad: HALLOWEEN /
  SEATING** (turned face down; "No," in harmony). Laney's FB: "political refugee on the fourth floor." Kevin: "a fire code violation and
  a cry for help." Laney's account: the Madonna Inn pink booth, she took the tin from the cardigan "in front of *him*." Laney's
  Halloween ideas for Idaho: "No."
- s07 (Wed 1:00, Hollis 302): **"Good afternoon, Mr. Costigan."** Hale: "Mr. Costigan has been demoted." Jer 17:9–10; Yusuf, Al-Anfal
  8:24. Papers swapped; rebuttals Monday; Hale's PHIL 1 midterm Thursday. **Lena's paper "That He Cannot":** Jer 17:9; 1 Cor 4:4;
  *mihi quaestio factus sum*; ST I-II q.112 a.5; Mark 14:29; O'Connor's letters; last line: "A woman who says she knows her own heart
  is either a saint or lying."
- s08 (Wed 6:20, Anacapa Commons): **Dow closes 10,015.86** (first time over 10k in a year). Theo's Martinelli's toast; Jack: "Tell
  Lordstown." Lena and Margot share waffles; Margot catches Jack looking.
- s09 (Wed 9:05, the Brick, 8th floor, open 24 hours for midterms): Lena at the window table, Jack mid-floor, twenty yards of carpet.
  Jack in Danny's Carhartt. **Becca's MIDTERM SURVIVAL — JACK bag** (granola bars, clementine, earplugs, Glory Be holy card, "You'll do
  great! I prayed. —B"); a second clementine for Lena; kiss on the cheek; "Ranch hours."
- s09b (Thu 12:20): Jack writes *Which are you?* beside her last line. Kevin: "So is a different floor." Priya on the overnight desk.
  **Rafi offers "Focus? Midterm special."** in the lot; Jack declines on every path.
- s10–s10v (Thu 4:00–4:16, duel **cor**): her red **THEN WHAT DID YOU TELL HIM?** beside his first sentence; 1 Cor 4:4. **Lena: "Wes
  told me."** She knows: Wes asked Jack to take care of her; [e9_take=="drink"] Jack "said yes with a bottle"; Jack told Wes about Danny
  ("It took me a pier"). "Y'all don't get to *assign* me… I'm not a parish." Peter, Mark 14:29. She does **not** know the ring or the
  attic. Verdicts per duel macros (see journal "record" card). Lena keeps Becca's clementine in her cardigan pocket.
- s11 (4:19): elevator: "We're good." / "We're *fine*." **Ferrer Tower: Facilities tape, the swollen steel door won't latch.** Lawn
  chair in the fountain.
- s12/s12s (4:26): "Don't." "I wasn't going to." **Lena goes under the tape first.** 160 steps (Jack counts on `e10_climb=="count"`).
  Ephesians running joke ("say a decade for you at the bottom").
- s13 (4:40, the playing cabin): Facilities sign AUTOMATIC SYSTEM ENGAGED 7 AM–10 PM · DO NOT OPERATE CLAVIER. **Jack gives Lena Danny's
  Carhartt** (she wears it through 7 a.m.). **The great bell: VIVOS VOCO · MORTUOS PLANGO · FULGURA FRANGO** (Lena translates). **Lena
  plays the basement piano** — at three in the morning since August, badly on purpose — two years of lessons at the Rosary (the nun and
  the ruler). Giants 5–0 at the Dome Sunday; Saints 4–0. "Who dat."
- s13p (Thu 5:00): **Lena plays "When the Saints Go Marching In" on the Ferrer carillon with her fists; one bell is flat on the third
  ("That's not flat. That's *New Orleans*.").** Anacapa's lights come on; someone in Delgado hollers WHO DAT. Theo sms 5:01 "Costigan.
  Is that the Saints." Campus police cruiser onto the lawn. `ferrer_bells`.
- s13a [hide]: behind the great bell; cop: "Nobody's dumb enough." Out at 5:40; fingernail moon rising. s13b [run]: across the Commons
  ("Hey —" at two backs unless `e10_play=="watch"`). Priya at the desk: "I didn't see you. The scanner's been broken since two."
  [e10_priya=="never"] "Go up. It's warm."
- s14 (5:50, 8th floor): still full night over the water. **Facilities chains and padlocks the tower door.** Lena gives Jack half
  of Becca's clementine. "Dulce has no palate." **Jack describes the First Way at Mariner Field at halftime — "Aquinas and football.
  That's the dream girl right there" — and doesn't hear that he has described Lena.**
- s15 (6:02–7:00): "Wes doesn't argue either. He says he out-waits me." **Lena asleep on Jack's shoulder 6:03–7:00.** Her LG lights
  WES at 6:09. Becca sms 6:41 "prayed for u at 2 like always." Jack's Jesus Prayer, unfinished. **At 7:00 the chimes wake her; the
  near-miss; Jack does not kiss her on any path.** Then "Midterm, Youngstown/Costigan." "Same one." "Sit somewhere else."
- s16 (Thu 9:30, Sandoval Hall 1): PHIL 1 midterm, essay: "Aquinas's First Way. State it; then break it." Hale: "the first thing that
  tower has done that I've enjoyed." **In the exam Jack hears what he said at dawn.** Lena in the aisle seat of Row M, Jack Row P.
- s16b (10:50): Becca sms 10:51 "12:10?? saving u a seat"; Theo sms 10:53 balloon; Kevin with his BlackBerry.
- s17a/s17c [mass]: memorial of St. Teresa of Ávila; Osei's "no wonder You have so few"; Lena beside Mrs. Fitch in the front pew, laughs.
  Becca: "Is Teresa the one with the angel?" Lena: first woman Doctor of the Church (1970). Becca's bat ray.
- s17b [fishbowl]: **Balloon Boy, Oct 15** (Fort Collins; down 12:35 PT near Platteville); Laney's pageant-host commentary; Theo's
  logline; Kevin stops his own bit; Margot: "too calm to have a child in it." Lena reads the crawl: **Obama in New Orleans** (UNO town
  hall). Laney moves her bag off the cushion; Lena doesn't sit.
- s18 (2:15, Fishbowl): the boy found in the attic over the garage. Laney's "defective" mascara. Jack thinks of Pauger Street. Evan's
  section out early; the Anchor Thursday 7–1.
- s19 (Fri 1:25 a.m., Delgado front steps): **Laney's Big/Little: Sloane Kittredge is her Big.** Paddle (LANEY / SLOANE / PI LAM LOVE).
  Pi Lam lore: pledges used to walk the Point wall at midnight on the outside — "They don't anymore. Since." (Sloane wouldn't say.)
  **Kaylee Whitmore** (see knowledge.md). The DRP pillbox. "I went in her *pocket*."
- s19c (1:52): Lena comes down barefoot; "Whose tin do you think that is?"; "I've got her, Jack." **Laney moves back to 217 Fri ~2 a.m.**
- s20 (Fri 12:30, 4th-floor lounge): **Tide Fri Oct 16 p.3, by Margot**: carillon story; Facilities "not amused"; **Hale quoted
  ("bad, and the first thing that tower has done I've enjoyed")**; the door chained. Whiteboard: SUSPECTS 1. A CATHOLIC 2. A DIFFERENT
  CATHOLIC 3. THE HOLY GHOST (Laney, pink). Margot names nobody. Lena carries Laney's things back to 217.
- s21 (Fri 9:40 p.m., Rialto, Becca's Ranger): *Where the Wild Things Are* (opened Oct 16); Becca cried twice; "And it was still hot."
  **Maria Costigan's thank-you note to Becca** on St. Brigid letterhead (Rosie's crab on the fridge by her grandmother's Mass card;
  Palmer method). [poem=knee] "Not yet." **WE SHOULD MATCH!!** list (Isidore & Maria; Max & a Wild Thing, 4-H lamb ears; cowboy & cow
  crossed out; ask Jack); Jack: "Joachim and Anne." **"You look at me like I'm homework." "I'll wait." "Due Halloween."**
- s22 (Sat 1:15 p.m.): **SAINTS & SINNERS invitation, Sat Oct 31, the Ferrante house, Del Playa Lane, halo or horns, "In honor of
  whoever played the Saints on the Ferrer Tower" —T.A.V.** Theo's over-specific denial (last year *Eyes Wide Shut*). Laney's FB "217
  4ever." **Purdue beat Ohio State Sat Oct 17;** Gina: "dad turned the radio OFF." Frank: "Purdue." "Thirteen-point favorite." **Nonna
  on the extension calls Jack "Danny," then knows him; "E la ragazza del granchio?"** Priya's HAPPY DIWALI laminate; a battery diya on
  Nora's box.
- s23/s23b (Sat 6:00): new brass padlock and AUTHORIZED PERSONNEL ONLY sign on the tower; the flat bell on the hour. Lena on the Brick
  steps in a blue sundress, five fingers: *Tomorrow.* (Sunday Saints–Giants.)

**Choice-dependent facts**
- s10b Confess (`e10_cor_truth`): Lena knows Jack didn't say yes — he drank and Wes took it for one. Personal (`e10_cor_personal`):
  Jack threw the last house on Seacliff at her; she goes formal ("Mr. Costigan"); without apology (`!e10_cor_apology`) the stairs climb
  in silence, she calls him "Costigan" at seven, and "Jack. Not Mr. Costigan." on the steps Friday.
- s17a Becca (`e10_becca_q`): seven (true, partial) / lena (Becca knows Lena was at the Brick all night with Jack, fighting about papers;
  she's glad) / two (`e10_lied_becca`: Jack told Becca he left the library at two — **a live lie**).
- s16b `e10_noon=="fishbowl"`: Jack stood Becca up at the 12:10; she mentions the saved seat at the Rialto (becca.trust −1).
- s19b Text: Jack texted Lena "steps. laney." / Wait: Lena heard Laney's laugh up the stairwell.
- s19 `e10_kaylee=="pillbox"`: Jack took Laney's DRP pillbox; it went into Lena's cardigan pocket against the tin.
- s13p run without lookout (`e10_seen_running`): a campus cop saw two backs running for the Brick; Margot quotes the police log ("One of
  them was laughing"). Hide or lookout-run leaves no police trace.
- s12s cigarette (`e10_cig`): **Reliquary No. 5 · 10/15/09 · Ferrer Tower · in the bells** (written on a Pacifica call slip with Jack's
  red pen).
- s15 `e10_dawn`: still (Jack holds; he sits back) / face (she sits back) / magdalena (he said her full name; she sits up, cold at s16).

**Ledger housekeeping (from the S1E11 review)**

### S1E12 — "All Hallows’ Eve" (Fri Oct 30 – Sun Nov 1, 2009; tag Sun 11:40 a.m. PST)
33 scenes. Forks: s07 → s07a widow's walk (`e12_where=="roof"`) / s07b caterers' kitchen (`kitchen`) → s08 · s09 → s09a Ocean Road
(`e12_becca=="walk"`) / s09b front steps (`door`) → s10. Duel **grace** ("A Good Man," s15a–s15v, Seacliff Road walking east,
**unofficial, no referee**; Lena, dressed as O'Connor, argues AGAINST violent grace — Thomist: *gratia non tollit naturam sed perficit* —
Jack FOR it — Augustine X.27, Matt 11:12 Douay). Extended Jack/Lena two-hander = Act Three (s11–s16). Per path ≈13.3k–14.2k words
(mean ≈13.7k; Act Three ≈3.8k–4.3k), 21 choices, 8–12 keys, 0–5 whispers (Lena ≤2, Becca/Theo/Laney ≤1; a pure-Control path gets the
s16 "Keep it on" whisper). Jack's resolve enters 10, ends **5–10**. Lena's resolve: s04 5 → s06a 5 → s08b 4 → s11 4 → s13 4 → s16 4 →

**Ledger housekeeping (from the S1E12 review)**
- **2:00 a.m. happens once** on Nov 1, 2009; only 1:00–1:59 repeats. Keys/journals anchor the hat to "the last minute of the extra hour."
- Transit: the last 11 bus is 12:30 a.m.; after a 1:00 Anchor close Jack rides La Flaca.
- Gina's Saturday-night texts are stamped Eastern (11:12 PM – 12:30 AM ET = 8:12–9:30 PM PT, during the party).
- Becca's touch-tank shifts are Thu afternoon + Fri 6 a.m.; she worked **Sun Nov 1 at 6 a.m.** only because the swine-flu volunteer is still out.
- Geography: Seacliff's Greek row (Pi Lam easternmost) is the Grove end; the Lighthouse is the west end; the Point is north of the
  Lighthouse porch. From Del Playa's cliff end you do not pass Pi Lam heading to the Lighthouse. Avoid compass words for the Point from
  the Ferrante roof ("the far rail").
- Philip Neri: Florentine by birth, "Apostle of Rome." The Anglican plan was announced Oct 20 (a fortnight before Nov 1); the apostolic
  constitution is Nov 4/9 — not yet.
- Sandoval Hall 1 is the lecture hall, not a residence.

can be wronged). No two-hander (E14 per the every-other rule); Lena central. Per path ≈13.2k–14.1k words (mean ≈13.7k), 22 choices,
6–12 keys, 1–4 whispers. Lena's resolve: s02 5 → s04 6 → s05 5 → s06 5 → s07 7 → s07d 7 → s09 6 → s11 **5 (ends 5)**. Jack's resolve
ends 2–10. No dangerous window. Heat: one near-miss (s07d, `e13_heat`=1, no kiss, no "I want you" aloud). **Save-locked:** written for

  kind."; push-ups 150; [e9_hey given] margin: ABOUT TIME. WHAT'D SHE SAY BACK. Letter kept in the Carhartt's inside (button) pocket,
  folded twice. Whiteboard: HALLOWEEN — AMERICAN GOTHIC (UNPLANNED) · BOTH; C 7 · B 6; pink pitchfork with a bow (Laney).
- s03 duel: Hale on time (fourth time this term), sober, linen jacket on the chair; whiteboard (Epicurus *Menoeceus*, Nagel "Death" 1970,
  *NE* I.10–11). Rule restated: **"The thing you least believe you can prove."** Lena has typed notes. Lena's moves: Aristotle/Ross "a very
  unfriendly doctrine," Nagel's betrayed man; Monica at Ostia ("Put this body anywhere…," unattributed translation); 2 Macc 12:46 Douay
  written on the board; "They gave my mother her medal back in a plastic bag in February." [Press] Hector, the antemortem person ("You
  can wrong him backward"). Yusuf: "We pray for them too. We're not allowed to stop." Caleb: in his dad's church the dead were the only
  people nobody prayed for. Verdicts V1–V5 (see record card). Hale, packing: "They can be wronged… You can't apologize to them." Lena
  carries the marker out and brings it back. V3b line now: "Lucretius would be proud of you. He never finished either."
- s04: St. Brendan's **Book of the Dead** (red ledger, Mrs. Fitch's pen on a cord, masking tape; ETERNAL REST cards with November Mass

  Lena writes *Lucille Guidry* and **Nora Calloway** ("She was my host at Preview Weekend. In April. Two days."). Osei: "Write them all.
  He has a large book and a long memory." Jack writes **Antonio Rinaldi** (`e13_book`: nonno/blank — a dot on an empty line/candle).

- s04a: **Julian Park-Ellis meets Jack and Lena** (Jack's name given; Julian already knew his face from Bastille). Julian writes Nora's name
  again in square capitals; "She prayed for people out of the newspaper." Sang with her freshman year (she had the high part). "She
  mentioned you." (to Lena). "You left in the second chorus." / "So did she." / "Everybody does. I should cut it." Something new Thursday.
  "I don't really — football." / "Nobody's asking you to, Julian." No narrator gloss linking "Fresno" to Nora.
- s05 Sully's: Dale (Barry Sanders jersey; Lions 1-6, beaten by the Rams Sun Nov 1). Lena went to the 12:10 for All Souls. Theo's Who Dat
  ("dialect coach — YouTube"), root beer for the bar; Margot: "a responsorial psalm with a point spread." Kevin: "a hemorrhage with a
  jersey number." Laney/Kevin napkin scores. Lena rings the bell five or six times. Phone: LUC, REMY, TESS, JOSIE, JUDE, LUC [whodat]
  DADDY. **No WES.** "I'm going to call him when we get back." "Go on, cher. I'm fine."
- s06/s06a: east stairwell, 2nd-floor landing; window open six inches; full moon square on the concrete. **No 8:04 call tonight.** Lena:
  "Sit on the stairs. Don't listen." The call: Mass since **September**; Pauger Street; the attic; "A little" (drinking); "A ring." "You'd
  marry me anyway." Ruth, Peter, "pray with me." "I love you too." **Wes ends it** (`lena_wes_over`).
- s07/s07t/s07r: "Don't say anything wise. I'll push you." Lena tells it in order: stopped in September; 8:04 every night; the woman in
  the attic, cypress rafters, the uncle's axe; four years untold; drinks "a little"; **Mémère's ring** (given Easter, "for when," in his
  desk drawer on Zimple Street); at the tomb on All Saints (St. Louis No. 3), Mémère: *Et la bague, Wesley? Quand?*; Josie texted in
  capitals. Wes: "You'd marry me anyway. That's why I can't let you." Won't stand at the back of the church at their children's baptisms.
  "He went somewhere I can't follow, and he won't come back where I am." (endCard tag). [ring told] s07r: "you let me find out from a
  thirteen-year-old"; "Was it pretty?" "It should be. She wore it every day of my life." "Thank you for telling me now."
- s07a: Mémère is 88, 4'10", a Buick "as long as a pew," checks tombs with a carpenter's level; the camcorder; the monks' fruitcake in
  her closet; St. Odilo of Cluny. "I knew in September. He stopped yelling." [empty_room] callback. "He used it." (the leaving argument).
  [cor_truth] "You didn't say yes — you drank." "I drank. I told you." "You saying yes now?" (`e13_care`). [tulane] "Not tonight, Jack.
  Funny later. Stay now."
- s07b: at three, Youngstown time/Nonna. "Tell me something true." (`e13_true`): Nonno (Sheet & Tube, Campbell Works, Black Monday Sept 19,
  1977; "the mill was the only church that ever laid him off"; Nonna tells him the Browns at the stove in Calabrese) / [cross] **Theo at
  the wall** ("He shouldn't be up there.") / Danny's line.
- s07c: 4:30 "Give me one." [give] **Reliquary No. 8**: the east stairs, 11/3/09, on the back of an All Souls card ("All Souls" anyway);
  she smokes half. [no] palm to palm. [pack] "Confiscated. Pending review."
- s07d **the dawn (the player's question, answered):** she cries at first light; the Salve Regina said, not sung; Jack picks it up at
  "Turn then"; her head on his shoulder; her fist in his shirt. He wants her and does not turn his head. **Why Jack stays with Becca:**
  (1) Wes held a door open; whoever walks through walks through another man's worst night; (2) "take care of her" = take nothing;
  (3) she wants to be *asked*, and you don't ask the night her house falls down; Jack is *sure* (his belief, not fact) she'd say yes to
  anything that held still, and he wants a yes from someone who could say no; (4) Becca will not be the girl left the minute somebody came
  free, and Lena will not be the reason anybody got left; he'll end it with Becca honestly, in due course; (5) the private one: free, he'd
  be on those stairs every night; Becca is "the fence he can see." **The catch:** to tell her what he's waiting for would be to ask her,
  so he can't tell her. Choice `e13_reason` (asked/care/fence/becca) + journal note "The wait."
- s07e: Priya at 6:05 (fleece, orgo, early lab) leaves a Nature Valley Oats 'n Honey bar. Sun 6:12. "Jack. What are you going to do?"

### S1E14 — "Say It to My Face" (Wed Nov 4 – Sun Nov 8, 2009; tag Sun 11:52 p.m. PST)
36 scenes (33 per path). Forks: s12 → s13a Caleb on the Shipwreck Cove stairs (`e14_vile=="caleb"`) / s13b "That's enough, Professor." (`hale`) /
s13c Hale's deck with Lena and Berkeley (`deck`) → s14 · s17a → s18a the Old Mission wedding (`e14_day=="mission"`) / s18b the harbor fish market
(`harbor`) → s19. Duel **face** ("Say It to My Face," s09–s11v, **official, Hale's table, Hale referees**; Jack = GO (Paul in Athens, Acts 17;
John 17:15), Lena = COME AWAY (Benedict per Gregory's *Dialogues*; MacIntyre's last line; Acts 17:32; Matthew 10:14)). **The scheduled extended
two-hander** (s15–s23, Fri 10:50 p.m. → Sun 2:25 a.m.): friendship only, **no heat set anywhere**. Per path ≈13.1k–14.1k words (mean ≈13.6k;
all branches ≈18.0k), 23 choices, 6–12 keys, 1–6 whispers. Lena's resolve: s01 5 → s04 4 → s06 5 → s08 5 → s12 4 → s15 5 → s17 6 → s19 7 →
s20 7 → s23 6 → s27 6 (**ends 6**). Jack's resolve enters 4, ends 4–9 (mean ≈7.2). No dangerous window. **Save-locked:** written for
`saves/after-s1e13.json` (`lena_wes_over`, `e13_reason=="asked"`, `e13_dawn=="need"`, `e13_caroline=="becca"`, `e13_ring` kept, `e13_julian=="stay"`,
`e13_gift=="moviegoer"`, `e13_glass=="wrote"`, `e9_hey=="given"`, `e11_vote=="vespers"`, `e4/e6_goodnight=="magdalena"`). Gates: Ripostes and s21/s22
[Wit] `trait.wit>=38` (save 38: visible); s15 candor branch and s22 [Candor] `trait.candor>=54` (save 53; s04 "told" +1 opens it); **s10 Personal
`e14_wine=="all" || trait.reckless>=28`** (save reckless 26 → only a Jack drinking Hale's claret sees it); s11 Apologize `e14_face_personal`.

**Clock (binding).** Wed Nov 4 noon Portolá steps (St. Charles Borromeo; WS Game Six that night); 1:00–2:10 seminar (papers up at 2; whiteboard
at 2:10); FB status 4:11/4:12 [not hedge]; 8:04–8:30 east stairs, 2nd-floor landing (no call; the hour that was Wes's). Thu Nov 5 1:12 a.m. Theo's
"Are you happy?"; 12:10 Mass; **Fort Hood** known by ~12:50 (Joey's father; "the number keeps going up"); rosary in the front pew; Jack's 1:02 text
to Yusuf, reply 2:14 (**Tariq hadn't called yet**); 6:20 p.m. the hotdish at 217. Fri Nov 6: **unemployment 10.2% (highest since 1983)** on the
Fishbowl TV in the morning; Jack doesn't call his father; Yusuf's brother called Friday morning; Hale's dinner 6:40 (sit-down 7:30; duel 8:10–8:55;
margins 9:20; forks 9:38; Becca leaves 10:45 for the 6 a.m. tank with Laney [and Caleb]); the walk 10:50 (moon four nights past full); Delgado front
desk 12:40 a.m. Sat Nov 7: 7:12 Mémère (9:12 in Breaux Bridge, home from St. Bernard's 8 o'clock); 7:40 "I need to not be in this building today";
11:00 the Old Mission wedding [mission] / 10:30 the harbor [harbor]; 3:30 confession (Lena eleven minutes); 5:00 vigil (32nd Sunday B: 1 Kgs 17:10–16;
Mark 12:38–44); 7:40 the Anchor; **Stupak 240–194, then the bill 220–215, Cao the only Republican yes** (after eight on KNX); LSU lost at Alabama;
Ohio State won at Penn State (Gina: PRYOR!!!); Margot's call at ten. Sun Nov 8: the last 11 bus left 12:30 a.m.; the three-mile walk; Delgado front
door **2:25 a.m.**; **Alarm 4 at 3:11** (east stair); Margot's laptop 3:40; Sully's 10:50 (**Saints 30, Panthers 20 → 8–0**: down 14–0 after one,
17–6 at the half, 20–13 in the third, Meachem 54-yd TD, Anthony Hargrove's 1-yd fumble return = the defense's 7th TD; late FG); Anacapa lot 1:20;
Maria 4:30 p.m. (7:30 Glenwood); 9 p.m. Mass (Kevin lector; Prayers of the Faithful from the Book of the Dead); courtyard wall 10:05; Danny letter
11:40; Lena's text **11:51 p.m.**

**On-page canon (all paths unless marked)**
- s01: Portolá front steps; "Hey, Soul Sister" from a laptop on the lounge sill. Becca's planner (St. Charles Borromeo: catechists and plague).
  **Becca's question:** "My mom's been calling you my boyfriend since September. Can I tell her she's right?" [e13_caroline becca] "You told Theo's
  *mom* I'm with you." Procedure: "My mom first. Then Facebook." **Becca flies SBA → Seattle → Spokane the Wednesday before Thanksgiving** (her dad
  drives from Coeur d'Alene with the dog); **the turkey is named Gerald**. **Jack is taking the Greyhound home, 2½–3 days** (Becca writes BUS?? in her
  planner). `e14_ask`: yes (she phones her mother; Mrs. Lindell: "Is that *him*?") / weight ("Yes," meant to mean it; her hand, a nick on one knuckle
  "from something that had hooves") / hedge ("ours till Thanksgiving"; "No Facebook. Mom doesn't count… She thinks it's a kind of mail"; maple bar
  halved) / good ("You're good at *me*"). `becca_official` on every path.

- s08: **Hale's house**: last lot, cliff end of Del Playa; silver board-and-batten bungalow; books to the ceiling; woodstove; **Bishop Berkeley**
  (deaf greyhound) on a Turkish rug; **the table is a six-panel door on trestles**; deck over the bluff; Billie Holiday. Menu card: standing rib, Yorkshire
