  [Riposte] "Athens hasn't had a good century since." — "Neither has Youngstown." / [Concede] "That's the part I can't prove." / **[Personal, drinkers
  only] "You'd know. You never ask anybody the question you're scared of."** (Lena, level, to Hale: Monica prayed seventeen years and let Ambrose do
  the talking). s11: Matthew 10:14 (Lena cites only Matthew's "no bag, no second tunic"). **"Some people you can't follow. You let them go, and you pray
  at a distance, and you leave the light on."** [Concede] "Leave the light on, then. You're still going. Just slower." (`e14_face_light`) / [Press] "The
  dust is for the town…" — "Tell that to the people in it." — "I'm telling *you*." / [Riposte] Gentile country; "Then pass the dust." / [Apologize].
  **Verdicts** (record before: 5–2): V1 personal → Lena 5–3 ("The point goes where the cost is") / V2 personal+apology → Lena 5–3 ("Retreat with honor
  is still retreat") / V3a light → Lena 5–3 ("Mr. Costigan conceded the only point worth having"; the cream jug) / V3a′ heart≥2 without the light →
  Lena 5–3 ("gave ground twice, and graciously") / V5 wit → **draw**, 5–2–1 / V4 rigor≥3 → **Jack 6–2** ("He went, and he came back with two") /
  V6 → Lena on points, 5–3.
- s12: Hale's Yorkshire coming up; the marginalia recited (Jack's first week "best paragraph… I hate it"; *Coward.*; Lena's *Cite me.*, *Defense.*);
  this week's margins; **Hale stops in the middle of Lena's margin** — "*You started another name on Monday and stopped. Whoever it is —*" — "Ah,"
  folds it away; "Illegible." **The postcards revealed:** "Two people at this table wrote to me before they ever sat in my seminar… A postcard each —
  same card, same line… I buy them at Blackwell's by the hundred. **I've used three.** Mr. Costigan's nine pages, from Ohio. Miss Broussard's
  twelve, from New Orleans." Caleb: "I wrote you four letters." Hale: "I keep them… I don't answer devotion, Mr. Hargrove. It doesn't need me."
  Caleb leaves **without his coat**; Hale to his glass: "Bloody boy" (Barnsley vowel). Lena to the deck with Berkeley. Becca: "Is he always like this?"
- s13a [caleb]: Shipwreck Cove stairs (unlit Christmas lights on the rail); Caleb's four letters: thanks (eleven pages), questions (footnotes), **his
  dad's church — what happened to it**, "did you get the third one." "My dad never answered either. He just *smiled*." / s13b [hale]: "That's enough,
  Professor." — "Yes. It is." … "You'll do, Mr. Costigan." Yusuf: "Factor of safety." / s13c [deck]: the bench, Berkeley between them; "He's the only
  one at this party with any sense."
- s14: Becca drives Laney (and [caleb] Caleb) home in the Ranger; [not caleb] **Becca takes Caleb's corduroy to find him**. Becca: "Walk Lena home?
  She shouldn't walk by herself." Hale at the door: **"I wrote to you both because you were the only ones who wrote *at* me like you meant it."**
  [hale] "Even you. *Especially*." He looks west toward the Point.
- s15 the walk: [personal && !apology] Lena courteous and one degree colder, walks with him anyway. [wine all] "You're tilting." "Ships list." "Ships
  have keels." Jack takes the street side (Frank's rule). **"You're being very careful with me, Costigan."** **Lena's postcard, behind her driver's
  license, soft as cloth.** "I asked you who A.H. was." "You read it twice." "I read it *once*. Then I read mine, in my head." **Lena wrote Hale twelve
  pages in spring '08, two years after the year she went to Mass every morning to yell at God (2005–06).** Jack: [candor≥54] the summer at sixteen he
  read Hale in three nights and quit Mass; drove Maria to St. Brigid's in the Silverado and sat in the lot; "Something happened that fall. I started
  again." (elided, unasked) / [else] "I was seventeen. I'd read him at sixteen and thought I'd found a hole." "He wrote back. That's a hole."
  **The Zimple Street house** (shotgun, fig tree, For Sale since the storm; the grandmother's sewing table; haint blue porch ceiling; babies at Holy
  Rosary; Mémère's ring; Sunday dinners on Dupre Street). `e14_grief`: press (**"Don't argue me out of it, Costigan. I'm not a case."**) / house
  (room by room; the Singer; books on all walls; the dog **Boudreaux** under the kitchen table; the claw-foot tub on the back porch under the fig:
  "It's *Uptown*. They've seen worse.") / quiet (her hand through his arm in the dark of the Grove).
- s16: Delgado front desk 12:40. **The two cards side by side**: Radcliffe Camera, BLACKWELL'S · OXFORD; *Come and say it to my face. —A.H.*; his to
  Glenwood Avenue, hers to North Dupre Street; both MARISOL CA 2008 — **hers March (26¢), his June (27¢, after the May rate rise)**; his has a Folgers
  half-ring. [moviegoer] Jack's card lives in his desk drawer since *The Moviegoer* went to Theo [else: *The Moviegoer* on his desk]. **Laney's 11:20
  sms: "she sang hymns the WHOLE way. i know all the words to be not afraid now. i hate it here"** (the ride home with Becca). `e14_cards`: swap (till
  Sunday; hers goes into *Big Sur*) / own / both (she keeps both overnight). "Goodnight, Jack."
- s17: 7:12 Delgado kitchen; **Lena picks chickens in the Newman stockpot for Sunday's soup (Harbor Inn night shift)**. **Mémère by phone** (speaker):
  the fruitcake came Friday, *trop sec*, a slice each morning thin enough to read the *Teche News* through; a candle for Wesley at St. Bernard's: "*C'est
  un bon garçon. Le Bon Dieu a pas fini avec lui.*" Then: *Quelqu'un devait demander* (untranslated; Jack thinks he knows what). "*Et le garçon de
  l'Ohio?*… Wesley said you're skinny." `e14_memere`: oui ("He has manners. Feed him, Magdalena.") / browns (1–7; the bye; "they fired the general
  manager on Monday"; "*Pauvre garçon*" — "the highest honor we have") / french (Mrs. Kovach, the language lab; "*Je suis… maigre?*").
- s17a: "I need to not be in this building today." "You pick. I'll go."
- s18a [mission]: **Old Mission Nuestra Señora del Mar**, a wedding mariachi on the steps; program: **Yesenia Montoya & Rubén Salazar, 7 de noviembre de
  2009**; the four-year-old ring bearer eats the pillow fringe (Lena laughs out loud in church); the arras (13 coins) and the lazo; **Lena cries at the
  lazo**; Jack's Anchor napkin (she keeps it); "In New Orleans we'd have a second line out the door"; out at the Lamb of God. / s18b [harbor]: sea lions
  (eleven) on the float; the fish market shed; Daddy's rules (clear eyes, red gills, smells of the sea); Lena haggles a red rockfish down a dollar a
  pound plus heads ("Lagniappe"); **the rockfish is for a Monday court-bouillon for Dulce and Rosario** (stored in Rudy's walk-in: LENA'S. MONDAY. FOR
  THE HARBOR INN.); Wes worked her father's counter at seventeen the summer after the storm, hopeless at filleting. "You can tell a man by how he holds a
  fish." She carries it herself.
- s19: confession line (Joey's laminated card from his grandmother; four minutes); **Lena eleven minutes**; Jack prays a decade for her. `e14_conf` go
  (Osei: **"Your penance is to let somebody help you this week."**) / pew.
- s20: vigil; Becca saves three seats (Becca, Jack, Lena); Peter Haddad reads Kings; Osei: "Jesus does not say she was sensible. He says she gave more
  than all of them." Becca holds Jack's hand at the Our Father; Lena's stay folded. **The second collection (St. Vincent de Paul) at the doors, Mrs.
  Fitch's basket.** `e14_mite` tips (the ANCHOR envelope, all of it) / five / pocket (bus fare too; Lena pays both fares on the 11; La Flaca on the rack).
  Becca: "Lena, go to the Anchor with him! Gloria will feed you!"
- s21: the Anchor; Rudy's transistor on KNX; Lena on the stool by the dish station with Hale's Monday packet and buttermilk pie. **Gloria: "So this is
  waiting."** (the only line naming the wait); Gloria takes his apron at nine. **Lena's speech:** Charity shut since the storm; her father's shrimpers
  uninsured; **"my congressman" (Cao: a former Jesuit seminarian, New Orleans East) voted yes because the amendment kept the money off abortion; "Both,
  Jack. In one bill."** Remy: LSU lost in Tuscaloosa ("in mourning till Easter"). **Margot's argument** (exchange plans can't cover abortion; insurers
  won't write two policies; riders) — Lena: "She *may* be right about the private plans. She's wrong about the baby." `e14_bill`: lena ("Write it down.
  Margot's going to want it for the board.") / margot ("Tell Costigan he's on the board.") / cao ("I have a congressman.").
- s22: 12:55 a.m., the three-mile walk with La Flaca (past the dark Borders and Blockbuster, Giorgio's). **Wes will be at the Broussard Thanksgiving
  table (every year since 2005; Luc's best friend); he carried the twins out of a second-floor window — Jude and Josie, nine then, Josie (13 now) bit
  him** (`knows_broussard_twins`, `knows_wes_thanksgiving`). Lena rides the rack down Ocean Road; **the Brick's second floor lit all night** (the E17
  plant). **"I'd go anywhere on the twenty-sixth. Anywhere nobody knows his name." / "Is Becca going home with you? To Youngstown?" / "She's going home
  to Idaho. Her mom named the turkey." / "Right. Of course she is."** Jack holds *Then come to Youngstown* a week so it won't be a consolation prize
  (`jack_plans_ask_lena`). `e14_invite`: lupes (carnitas; "the pig is the most Catholic animal there is, because nothing on it goes to waste") /
  costigans (lasagna beside the turkey; Nonna's fried cardoons; Rosie's hand-turkey place cards; the Lions; **Danny's chair, set every year**) / quiet.
  "Are you going to be okay? At that table?" "I'll let you know."
- s23: Delgado front door 2:25 a.m., the red card-reader light. **"You've been a very good friend to me this week, Jack."** (endCard tag). `e14_door`
  job / magdalena / hold.
- s24: **Alarm 4, Sun Nov 8, 3:11, east stair (not the lounge), 26 days**; Margot staked out the 4th-floor lounge pull station with Theo's camera and Gold
  Bond; **she fell asleep 2:50–3:11 (first documented instance)**; footage: at 3:09 a bare heel and a pillow corner cross the frame (the only tell;
  unremarked). Laney: "Tell me I meet a dark stranger. Tell me he has a boat." Theo: "One woman. One camera… Wrong *stairwell*." Kevin: "That's REM."
- s25 Sully's: Dale (Barry Sanders jersey as a vigil; Lions 1–6); **Theo's seating napkin (COSTIGAN · BROUSSARD) redrawn by Lena** ("Becca goes by
  Jack… I want Margot"); Theo's courtesy to Becca ("the good seat"); Margot: "a potlatch." Becca came from the 6 a.m. touch tank. **Lena teaches Becca the
  Who Dat** ("You're not asking anybody, Becca. You're telling the room."); Becca: "It's a *holler*. We have one for the cows." **Becca's WHO DAT half a
  second early on the Meachem TD**; Kevin's napkin 10 *(early)*; Laney: "won the swimsuit round in a *Carhartt vest*." Lena rings the bell for the
  defense's seventh TD. No WHO DAT text from Wes. **The *Sweetwater* promo on the fifth TV (unnamed, "something bright and blue with water in it");
  Theo: "God. They're still running that."** Jack doesn't look. Becca: "Are you really doing three days on a Greyhound by yourself?" `e14_becca_tg`:
  told (**Jack tells Becca he means to ask Lena to Youngstown; Becca zips her lips: "It's yours to ask. I'm not *allowed*."**; `becca_knows_lena_ask`)
  / bus (BUS — JACK on her napkin; a survival bag promised).
- s25a: the Odyssey's back glass WHO DAT · 7–0 in La Vang polish → `e14_glass` wrote (Jack's leaning 8) / becca (Becca rewrites it all, WHO DAT · 8–0;
  Lena hands her the remover to keep).
- s26: Maria (picks up before the first ring ends): "Idaho! Your *girlfriend*!" [not hedge] Gina saw the FB status Wednesday [hedge] **Becca's card on
  the cow stationery — a thank-you for Maria's thank-you note, signed *Love, Becca*** ("A girl doesn't write *love* to the mother unless"). "Nonna wants to
  see the girl who writes on cows." **"Then Christmas! Bring her at Christmas. I'll make the cardoons."** (`maria_wants_becca_xmas`); the bus, three days.
  Nonna on the extension: "E la ragazza?" **"Your father's in the garage a lot."** `e14_ma`: see / **lena** ("The veil girl? Gina's friend, from the
  Facebook? Doesn't she have a *fidanzato*?" "Not anymore, Ma." — "Then I want both… One at a time, like a civilized family."; `maria_knows_lena_maybe`)
  / nonna (*Chi lascia la via vecchia per la nuova…*; "Giacomino").
- s27: 9 p.m. Mass; Becca (early lab Monday) and Lena either side; the Sunday soup went to the Harbor Inn at seven in Kevin's Odyssey. **Kevin, lector,
  reads the November names from the Book of the Dead** (Margaret Dwyer; Abuelita Rosa and Abuelo Chuy Ortega; Fr. Patrick Kelleher; Capt. Joe Silva of
  the *Marisol Rose*; Lucille Guidry; Nora Calloway; **Antonio Rinaldi**; Nora Calloway again). **Julian Park-Ellis stands inside the doors with his
  guitar case at the first Nora, and is gone by Communion.** "Be Not Afraid": Becca every verse from memory; Lena the alto.
- s27a: courtyard wall 10:05 (sprinkler shut off at the valve). **Julian found Lena on Facebook Tuesday; she opened her laptop this morning; he asked her
  to Bastille Thursday Nov 12 to hear the new song — it wasn't ready Nov 5, he pushed it a week — "He said you promised to stay to the end."**
  (`lena_julian_bastille`). [swap/both] the cards go home. `e14_bastille` there ("He thinks everybody leaves in the second chorus") / becca ("clapping on
  one and three") / go ("I think I will.").
- s28: Danny letter on a legal pad (Saints 8–0; "Browns had the bye. I'm told it went well."; **Nonno's name read aloud — "That one was yours."**);
  [e9_hey] ABOUT TIME margin. `e14_danny` hey ("That's all you get, jagoff." — Danny's word first) / truth ("Her boyfriend broke up with her. I'm with
  Becca now. Officially. I'm doing it right, D.") / pushups.
- s28a: **11:51 p.m. Lena: "Ask me again in a week. —L."** (answers "Are you going to be okay? At that table?"); Jack: *i will*.

**Owed payoffs (flag → episode):** `e14_mite=="tips"` → E15 (the housing deposit is short by a week's tips) · `e14_happy=="after"` → Theo's
calendar reminder after Thanksgiving (E18/E19; rhymes with E21's warzone) · `e14_bastille` → E15 (**Jack attends Bastille on every value**) ·
`e14_invite` colours the E15 tag (lupes: the joke he almost makes; costigans: the sentence he stopped before; quiet: the week he kept) · `e14_ask` and
`becca_knows_lena_ask` → E15–E16 (Becca must not tell Lena; she may ask Jack "Did you ask her yet?" only out of Lena's hearing) · `maria_wants_becca_xmas`
→ E16 (Maria writes Becca directly) · `maria_knows_lena_maybe` → E15–E16 (Maria's E16 call: "And the friend? Lena?") · `e14_becca_tg=="bus"` → E17 SBA
(the survival bag, for a plane) · `e14_danny` → E18 (Trumbull) · `e14_conf=="go"` → Osei's penance ("let somebody help you this week": E15's money
fork) · `e14_cards=="swap"` → closed in s27a.

**Continuity notes.** The Benedict holy card now lives in *Big Sur* (s09). Jack's Hale postcard: desk drawer [moviegoer path]. Hale has used **three**
cards (the third's owner is not named or counted on the page). Lena's yelling year = 2005–06; her letter = spring 2008. Becca's tank shifts: Thu
afternoon, Fri 6 a.m., plus the flu volunteer's weekend (Sat/Sun 6 a.m.); Monday = an early lab. The Brick's reading room is lit all night (E17's
meeting place). Journal cards for Becca, Lena, Danny and the duel record are duplicated per choice — any correction must be made in every copy.

### S1E15 — "Due Course" (Mon Nov 9 – Thu Nov 19, 2009, 12:25 a.m. PST)
> Written for the after-s1e14 save (wit 40, candor 56, reckless 28, sincerity 104; `e14_conf=="go"`, `e14_mite=="tips"`, `e14_day=="harbor"`, `e7_doubt=="twoam"`,
> `e8_date=="seals"`). Branch tags are `[scene | condition]`. Final per-path length 16.3k–17.4k words; 24–25 choices per path.

- s01 Mon 8:50 a.m., Delgado front desk: CNN, Berlin dominoes (1.5 km chain, Reichstag → Potsdamer Platz; **Wałęsa pushes the first at the Potsdamer Platz end; the chain
  runs toward and finishes at the lit Brandenburg Gate**). **Priya's pink notice: RESIDENTIAL SERVICES — NOTICE OF BALANCE DUE, SPRING 2010 HOUSING, COSTIGAN, JOHN A.,
  DELGADO 412, $340.00 after aid; REGISTRATION HOLD; due Fri Nov 13, 5:00 p.m.** **Residential Services** is the billing window (Housing/Residential Life's cashier office;
  distinct from Residential Life, which sends RAs the hold list). Freshman registration Mon Nov 16, 7 a.m.; Hale's spring seminar fills by 7:05. The Brick's circulation
  desk lost two overnight workers to swine flu: midnight–6, **$8.40/hr**. Margot wiped the 4th-floor whiteboard **all but one corner** (the useful-idiots tally lives in the
  corner: [bill lena] **C 8 · B 7**, STUPAK: SAME SIDE (BOTH) — E13 and E14 each added a point to E12's C 7 · B 6). The envelope lives in Hale's reader (Hume, miracles).
- s02 4:30 p.m.: Becca's FB wall post (box at the Newman desk). Theo knocks the envelope out of the reader; "I spend that on *parking*"; "very Steinbeck"; **"The offer
  has no expiration date."**
- s03–s04v **Duel `wall` "On the Wall" (unofficial; the Hale record card is NOT changed).** Whiteboard columns GORBACHEV (LINDQVIST) · THE POPE (COSTIGAN) · A UNION
  (BROUSSARD) · HASSELHOFF (VANCE). [harbor] Lena's court-bouillon in the Newman stockpot (LENA'S. MONDAY. FOR THE HARBOR INN.). Margot: Gorbachev "declined to do
  something catastrophic"; "Neither the Pope nor the electrician had an army. Gorbachev had one, and he kept it home." Jack: Warsaw June 1979. Lena: Gdańsk Aug 1980,
  **Wałęsa (fired from the Lenin Shipyard in 1976) climbs *in***; ten million in a country of 35 million. Lena's golden arches in yellow highlighter. [told_lena_frank]
  "ask your daddy who won. GM took a bailout and he took a pink slip." Moves: Press (altar at every gate; "the market never needed one") / Riposte (trendsetter; "Write
  down 'both'") / Concede (Frank would've joined; the Browns) / Reframe (Leipzig Oct 9, 70,000 candles; Sindermann "candles and prayers" — "*Source.*") / Personal
  ("Leave my father out of it", `e15_wall_personal`) / Apologize (`e15_wall_apology`). Lena's close: "prayer *and* bread. That's the whole Church." **Verdicts on Margot's
  board** per `e15_wall` (both / jack / lena) and the scores; [personal && !apology] "Night, Costigan." (not *Youngstown*) — one night only; Lena repairs it herself in s09.
  The verdict stays on the board through Wed Nov 18 (the orgo group uses the other half).
- s05 Tue 1:02–1:51 a.m. PST (4:02–4:51 EST): Maria calls; **the SUB pay ended last week; Frank went to the bar on Midlothian for MNF (Steelers at Denver), bar closed 2:30,
  truck not back; cell on the counter by the toaster**. Nonna dizzy on the stairs Sunday, sleeping in the front-room chair since. Gina on the landing. "I'm not calling the
  police on your father, Jack. I'm calling you." `e15_one_am` pray (Sorrowful Mysteries; Nonna in Italian) / talk (Rosie's crab in a hat; the cardoons; Becca's cow letter
  in the drawer with the good scissors) / candor (**"Don't wait up."** — "Twenty-six years… never said that to me"). **4:51 EST the Silverado hits the trash can; Frank sat
  in the Lordstown employee lot "to see if the lights were on."** "The spring thing. For your room." "I've got it, Ma." Theo lifted his head when the phone lit and put it
  back down (`knows_frank_drinking`). s05a 2:06: the landing window; **four Reds left**.
- s06 Tue 3:40 p.m., Mission Street P.O. (Fort Hood memorial on the radio): **money order No. 3 to D. KOSTELNIK, STRUTHERS, OH, $20 (+$1.10 fee), FROM blank**. Rafi
  (box to his other abuela in OXNARD — "a cold war"): "Pink's money"; **one ride, Thursday, $200**; "Vitamins, boss. Not bars." Rafi's number on the back of the receipt
  (Jack keeps three money-order stubs behind his Mooney ID). Marcus Aurelius "meant rent."
- s07 Tue 5:30, Newman lounge: **Mrs. Lindell's box** (Coeur d'Alene postmark): letter on the cow stationery ("Becca tells me you are her boyfriend now, which we knew");
  molasses cookies; **gray wool "North Idaho socks"**; **two jars of huckleberry jam: JACK and FOR THE CATHOLIC ROOMMATE**. Becca sees the pink notice: **Daisy's money,
  $612** (sold at the Kootenai County Fair, Aug 2008; credit union in Coeur d'Alene) (`becca_offered_daisy`). `e15_daisy` gently / joke / truth. Theo shelves his jar beside
  the espresso machine next to the birthday jar ("a supporter, not a member").
- s08 Tue 9:40, the Anchor: Rudy on KNX: "I know what an errand is, mijo." Gloria: "I can front you against December, hon" — refused. **Gina 10:02–10:19 p.m.: said Ashley
  Vrabel's father's DUI in religion; Ashley cried; Mrs. D (her teacher) gave detention and called Maria; "i heard the truck hit the can"** (`knows_gina_detention`).
  `e15_gina` sincerity / wit / candor ("ur not carrying it g") / control (3:50 alarm; calls her at 6:50 EST over Cheerios).
- s09 **Wed Nov 11 (Veterans Day; St. Martin of Tours), 11:20 a.m., the bench above Campus Point** (by the Marine Lab flagpole — NOT the Seacliff "bluff bench" across
  from Pi Lam). **Reliquary No. 9: "No. 9 · Campus Point · 11/11/09 · Veterans Day"** on the back of the 12:10 worship aid. St. Martin's cloak ("everybody forgets the
  beggar took it"); geese. `e15_bench` frank (**Tony Broussard gutted houses after the storm, not drank: "Some men drink and some men gut houses. Yours hasn't got a house to
  gut, Jack."**; "Call him Saturday… talk about that"; Jack shows her the hold; "I've got forty dollars" [harbor] "and a court-bouillon"; `lena_knows_frank_night`) / gina
  (Lena reads Tuesday's thread only; Sister Mary Margaret, Courtney Dugas, the Louisiana Purchase) / thursday (Wes nine days; she still sits on the east stairs at 8:04;
  [memere oui] Mémère called Sunday; "He thinks everybody leaves"). **Jack works Hector's Wednesday so Hector works his Thursday** (Jack free for Bastille).
- s10 Thu 1:10 a.m., under the Hobart: the Folgers can — [tips] **$154** / [else] well short. [conf go] the penance. `e15_money` rafi / theo / priya.
  - s11a [rafi] Thu 2:00 behind Delgado → 2:15 the harbor: a Vans shoebox in blue painter's tape on La Flaca; **Ray Ochoa outside the Anchor: "Saint Isidore. New
  shoes?" — he doesn't look in the box**; the Wharf Rat's back door (a man in a Raiders cap; "Rafi says gracias"); **$200 in twenties**; 3:55 Residential Services
  window ($140 from the can + $200), **Jack cuts Evan's Thursday section** (`jack_ran_errand`).
  - s11b [theo] 9:05: Theo in a navy blazer (Poppy's recital in La Cañada at two, dinner after); the ugly card; "One of them has a ferret"; **receipt reverse: $340.00 + 1 PIE
  (PEACH) —T.A.V.** (`theo_paid_deposit`).
  - s11c [priya] 9:00: **Hardship extension RS-12 (Section 4.2), laminated; due date moved to Dec 1 pending payroll; Jack fills in his mother's maiden name RINALDI**;
  **night desk at the Brick Thu and Fri this week, midnight–6, $8.40/hr; "Eight shifts, after withholding"** (`jack_brick_desk`). Priya buys sleeves at the Staples on Mission.
- s12 Thu 8:10, Café Bastille: Laney's napkin judging (TALENT / POISE / EVENING WEAR); Theo in La Cañada (sent Jack a photo of his blazer at six). **Yusuf's 19th birthday**
  (his friend announced it); Lena leads "Happy Birthday"; "a Catholic potluck in *café* form"; Yusuf watched the Fort Hood memorial standing up. **Julian ninth on the board;
  the song with no second chorus** (C string tuned down; the 51B at midnight). Lena's coffee goes cold. Jack stays to the end. "You stayed." "I said I would."
  `e15_stayed` good / cut ("a repeat sign") / she ("I saw").
- s13 10:32: Julian: "Can I walk you back?" "Yes." Laney: "Lovely set, Julian." The 11 (guitar case in the aisle). At the Anacapa stop Lena and Julian go in at Delgado's
  door; **Laney walks Jack round the far side of the Lagoon: "Walk me home, fireman. Apparently I'm available."** `e15_laney` escort (Miss Dallas County, baton,
  "literacy. And revenge.") / ask ("I am Miss Congeniality. I have the sash.") / quiet. [priya] the Brick, 11:55, the night desk.
- s14 **Fri Nov 13, 12:40 p.m., the Grove: Lena tells Jack, mid-sentence about monarchs, that Julian asked her to dinner (Saturday; tablecloths; "He asked if he could ask
  me something first. Then he asked.")** (`knows_lena_julian`). Lena knocks on wood and crosses herself; Philip the Fair, Fri 13 Oct 1307. The first monarch on a low branch.
  Jack: John 21:18 as grace; Lena: "Augustine with the cough taken out." Cousin Vinnie came back from Phoenix. All Souls = 11 days before. "The shape of the word *wait*."
  `e15_julian` charm / sincerity / wit (nickel package) / candor ("Are you happy?" — "I'm — Yes." + medal) / restraint ("I knew you'd be happy for me").
- s15 Fri 4:35, the Lighthouse back porch (Washed Out, *Life of Leisure*, Sept 2009): keg FRIDAY THE 13TH; Jack's bits (Templars; the keg as sacramental, toasted not blessed;
  the Youngstown liturgical year; St. Christopher off the calendar 1969). **Theo: "That's three bits in a row. You don't do three."** **Julian alone at the Point wall by
  the white cross, guitar case open** (unexplained). Theo's Corona; Jack leaves it on the rail.
- s16 Fri 10:15, 4th-floor lounge: orgo group (Priya's 2007 flashcards); LDA = St. Jerome, Grignards = Jesuits. **Garrett (Bio 1A, Visalia): "I think Maine got it right.
  Marriage is for kids." Kevin: "Biology doesn't vote, Garrett. People do."** Kevin's smile back crooked; "Getting water." `e15_kevin` follow → s16a: **Kevin to Theo by the
  roof door: "You said you'd never —" / "I haven't."**; Theo: "We were discussing Doug's raid schedule. He's a Tauren druid."; Kevin's hand on Jack's shoulder / stay →
  s16b: Jack turns the room (himself as a Grignard; Garrett's comeback); 11:05 he calls Glenwood (machine, recorded the year Rosie was born); hears the same two lines from

### S1E16 — "A Good Man" (Thu Nov 19 – Sat Nov 21, 2009; tag Sat 5:45 p.m. PST)
> Written for the after-s1e15 save (wit 41, reckless 29, sincerity 112, candor 59; `e15_her=="yes"`, `e15_conf=="go"`, `e15_frank=="rose"`, `e14_invite=="lupes"`,
> `e12_whos=="writer"`, `e13_caroline=="becca"`, `e15_coffee=="chorus"`, `theo_paid_deposit`). Branch tags are `[scene | condition]`. Final per-path length 19.0k–19.6k
> words (Skye ceiling ~22k); 25 choices per path; 7 whispers in the file. Player's E16 design round honored (Montecito party; the Pacific dare; the kiss soaking wet on
> the sand; a confused Theo at dawn; the invitation that day).

- s01 Thu 2:00 a.m., 412: Becca's 2 a.m. text ("its 2. praying for u like always. thats not a girlfriend thing."); [daisy gently] "daisy says hi btw. shes staying put."
  [go] Jack says *Becca* into the dark — the penance (every day till Advent, Nov 29). FB still *In a Relationship with Becca Lindell*; Joey's FINALLY BRO. [two_am reckless]
  three Reds left in the Carhartt. The clean mug outside 217, handle out.
- s01b 12:10, St. Brendan's: Maccabees; Luke 19:41–44 (Jesus weeps over the city); Osei ninety seconds ("the way a doctor cries in his car"; "Later is also an hour").
  Lena saves the 4th pew right with her bag. [go] Becca's name inside the Our Father. **Sign of peace: Becca crosses the aisle to Jack; [her yes] then to Lena.** Becca
  to the touch tank (Thursdays).

  COSTIGAN: NO; eight pages, Mon 9 a.m. Laney: "lost Miss Teen Dallas to a baton twirler"; `e16_laney` becca/quiet/wit; her open Diet Coke; "instructed to be nice to
  Becca. Effective today." Theo on the Anacapa lawn watching through the glass.
- s03 7:20 p.m., the Anchor: Gloria unties his apron and sends him home (lost six hours at $8); pecan slice in a to-go box.
- s04 8:50: Theo's TEAM JACOB tee under a wet-sand cashmere ("Delaney's orders. For deniability."); lobby 11:40.
- s05–s09r **The two-hander, Thu 9:00–11:40 p.m., the Delgado basement laundry.** The upright (dead F; "Dead since before Priya"); Priya's CLEAN THE LINT TRAP sign; 8
  washers, 6 dryers, vending machines, the clock in a wire cage. Lena: gray Rosary sweatshirt; Laney's *New Moon* (TEAM JACOB in silver Sharpie; Post-its pink = swoon,
  yellow = problematic, green = Lena objecting); page 400; "reading it out of spite"; "Leviticus had more plot." Julian plays Bastille till 11, picks her up 11:40.
  **Jack read Gina all four Twilight books on the Glenwood porch the summer after Danny went in** (Gina 14 then; *Breaking Dawn* midnight release at a Boardman strip
  mall; Frank: "That's not how you turn a double play"; Gina's first laugh since January). Jack's favorite: *Eclipse*, the tent. Gina is Team Edward ("Jacob is literally
  a dog"). **Duel `twilight` "Team Edward" (unofficial; Lena scores, lena pips 1→3).** Lena's thesis: the boy says no; "Decide for her and call it love"; Sister Agnes
  (Team Charlie) confiscated eleven copies. Verdict `e16_twilight` both (DRAW, in her capitals inside Laney's cover) / jack / lena ("A joke's a forfeit, Youngstown").
  [Concede s07a] "He should have asked her." (`told_lena_should_have_asked`). The Press line is about Edward ("He thinks he's the danger"), **not** Jack's own reason
  (reserved for S2E10). s08a **Lena plays "Clair de lune" properly, around the dead F** (`knows_lena_piano_played`); "If you play well, people ask you to play"; the
  last time she played it right in front of anybody was a November four years ago (🔒 Lucille's funeral). [e14_grief house] Jack thinks of Zimple Street. s09 the dead
  F bit ("Carnegie Hall"); their fingers on the same key. `e16_bench` restraint ("Heart and Soul," bass half) / candor ("This was a good night." — whisper) / **reckless
  (s09r: her fingers on his; she draws the fallboard down; resolve.lena → 4, which now carries — s09j/s11 no longer reset it)**. s09j 11:40 Julian at the top of the
  stairs (cardigan, car keys, BART-sticker guitar case). Her dryer empty.
- s10 Fri 12:01 a.m., the Rialto (Mission St.; the big house, old proscenium): NEW MOON 12:01 · SOLD OUT (an O borrowed from a 6). Laney's head counts; Theo, Margot
  ("a fertility rite"), Doug, Kevin (drove: "the designated adult"), Jack. Laney's song-title list folded in her clutch (from Julian's set). Theo: "Edward, with a capo."
  `e16_capo`. s10a Lykke Li "Possibility"; **Lena's head on Julian's shoulder around December**; Laney grips Jack's arm; out 2:20 a.m.; [chorus] Julian put the second
  chorus back. **Jack walks home down Mission Street, three miles, 2:30 a.m.**
- s11 Fri 5:10 p.m., 217: Gina's TEAM EDWARD text (12:40 p.m.); Laney's 3:12 text ("lena is DYING"). **Lena has a cold (99.something)**; H1N1 flyer on the door.
  **Two soups: Julian's mother's juk in a dented steel thermos; Gloria's chicken and rice (PECAN on masking tape) goes on the sill "for tomorrow."** **The Manhattan
  Declaration (released Fri Nov 20): Lena signed online, "for the last part" (religious liberty).** Margot: "drafted by men with chancery offices and very good lawyers";
  the marriage section in the same document, under the same pledge, as the unborn child; "coalition maintenance dressed up as moral philosophy." Julian: "What would you
  go to jail for?" Lena: the Claiborne overpass; a girl keeping her baby; a nurse; "Not for a bishop's feelings." `e16_decl` sign (`jack_signed_manhattan`) / press /
  concede (signed; `jack_signed_manhattan`).

- s13 9:40 p.m., 412: **Theo cancels the Harbor Inn table (Sat 8:00, COSTIGAN)** — "My mother can't come up." (`harbor_cancelled`; false dawn #2 dead). Theo's charcoal

  white to the service." "She feels you're fragile." Laney takes the lotion Kleenex to Lena. Jack is eighteen ("Details").
- s14 **9:55 p.m. (corrected from the blueprint's 10:40)**: the SL down the 101 south, top down, Randy Newman "I Love L.A." via cassette adapter; the better part of an

  Boat"); his father produced three bad films for Richard's company (the company is **not** named on the page); the house the second one built (a golden retriever in
  the Senate). Two black cars with LA dealer frames, drivers in them.
- s15 10:55, the Halloran house pool deck: glass and board-formed concrete over a canyon; the Channel view; Passion Pit "Little Secrets"; ~40 people. Theo lost in three
  minutes (Jordan's callback; Wyatt and the neighbor's noise call — "Wyatt, I'm nineteen."; a fedora's vampire-in-finance pitch). "Don't talk to anybody with a headshot."
  "Who reps you?" "The Diocese of Youngstown." Theo holds one full bourbon all night (driving). `e16_drink` reckless (Wyatt's Patrón) / control (one Pacifico) / restraint
  (a Coke; "You're very *Midwestern*").
- s16 11:50, the den: two Marisol sophomores on the stairs — "it's *Cassidy*" (one stranger's mention; never glossed). Den: beige carpet, a sectional, a TV with a score
  crawl, a birch-stacked fireplace, floor-to-ceiling glass over the canyon, a window seat. **Skye: man's flannel, cutoffs over black tights, barefoot, braided anklet, boots
  standing by the seat, sun-streaked salt hair, freckles, Sharpie in her teeth, the Harvest paperback of *A Good Man Is Hard to Find*, LIAR ×3 beside the grandmother.**
  No drink. **Jack crosses the room himself** (`e16_cross`; first time in the series).
- s17–s19v **Duel `misfit` "The Misfit" (unofficial; Skye the opponent; no Hale record card).** 11:55 p.m.–12:40 a.m. (`met_skye`). She is reading for a preacher's
  daughter (17; a tent; forgives a collection-plate thief in the rain; "very O'Connor," the director says; shoots in about a month). Her margins: LIAR; "only honest man in
  it"; LAST CARD. SHE'S BEGGING. PLAY IT AS A CON. Pitty Sing, Red Sammy's monkey, the navy straw sailor hat with white violets. Her bits: librarian, the grandmother,
  director in a scarf, the Misfit, a tent preacher (Reframe), "Swedish" at a guy in a visor; verdicts as a ring announcer / weather girl / museum audio guide. "I listen
  to how people breathe." Jack: "Like a thing that happened to me once." [writer] "I came as her. I never said she was right." `e16_misfit` jack_heart ("Nothing. That's
  the point." — she strikes CON, and [heart>=3] writes something small he can't read) / jack (cites O'Connor's essay; `jack_cited_oconnor`) / draw / skye. MISFIT boxed
  inside her front cover. Intermission: she reads the sectional (the couple who broke up an hour ago).
- s20 12:45: **"Jack Costigan" — she has his whole name (`skye_knows_surname`); she gives "Skye. Just the one."** "Do you go to Marisol?" "Not yet." **"I act. I've been
  working since I was eleven."** (`knows_skye_acts`) — braces at "In what?"; **"I don't have a TV, so it won't mean anything."** (her real laugh, through the nose). The
  back-pocket book habit ("You've done it four times"). The dumped-somebody face; "Wednesday." "She's the nicest person I've ever met." "Then you did it for her." [writer]
  "*a friend of mine* like it was in italics" (`skye_clocked_lena`). `e16_friend` control ("She's a friend." — "You're a terrible actor, Jack.") / candor ("She's seeing
  somebody.") / wit (O'Connor's been dead since 1964).

  want to leave?" `e16_theo` control (**text to Theo 1:04 a.m.: "got a ride. dont wait"**) / reckless (no text). Out through a door behind the bar fridge, the garage
  stairs, Wyatt's father's second car under a cover.
- s21 1:10, **the Saab: a 1991 900 convertible, red sunned pink, a hip-sized dent in the passenger door, one taillight out, duct-taped top; key in the floor; the deck stuck
  on *Rumours* since August (Fleetwood Mac "Dreams"); top down, heater full ("You heat your feet and freeze your face").** "I don't drink where there are phones." "It's
  GM." (GM 50% 1990, rest 2000; **the Koenigsegg sale pending — "nobody back home thinks it'll go through"**; it collapses Tue Nov 24, E17). Lordstown, Local 1112,
  Cavaliers then Cobalts, laid off since June. `e16_dad` candor (the Monday-before-last lot night; `told_skye_frank`; whisper) / control (the garage, *Go Bucks*).

  ever told me about their dad without wanting me to *do* something with it."** The Marisol exit; Ocean Road west past campus; the old coast highway; "Anybody?"
- s22 **2:15 a.m., Ellery Beach** (a dirt turnout four miles up the coast from the Lagoon, a gap in a wire fence, a surfers' path, the black stumps of a 1920s oil pier
  in two rows, the platforms lit offshore). **She names it: "Ellery. Surfers and me. The pier was for oil, in the twenties. Now it's for me."** Theo's 2:15 text [control]
  "Costigan. You're a grown man. Text me if you're dead." / [reckless] "where are you". She carries her boots down and leaves them at the foot of the path. **The dare:
  "It's fifty-seven degrees… I dare you. In your clothes." "Clothes is a commitment, Jack."** `e16_water` reckless (Lake Milton at eleven) / control ("Coat check") / wit
  (she goes under first). No moon; Orion high in the south, the Pleiades.

- s22b 2:40: the striped Mexican blanket from her trunk (surf wax, somebody's dog); [reckless] Theo's 2:50 text arrives on her boots while she is up at the car — **Skye
  never sees THEO on his screen.** Feet under his thigh ("a first date"; "I'll give you a credit"). "You have a hat face." **The St. Michael medal; the prayer's first
  line; "Snares… That's a beautiful word."** "I was baptized. Allegedly. There are pictures. A lot of lace." `e16_faith`.

- s23k **3:03, the kiss, soaking wet on the sand** (`kissed_skye`; resolve.jack −1; skye heat +2): salt, her cold hands on his neck, "Hi." / "Hi."; over sideways into
  the sand; the tide finds his jeans. Kissing only. `e16_stop` **restraint (s23a: Jack stops — the St. Michael cold at his throat; "You stop. Okay."; `jack_stopped_first`;
  the Saab with the top put up by hand, windows fogged)** / **reckless (s23b: she stops, as the grandmother — "In case of an accident, everyone should know I'm a lady."; top

  the fan page at 4,206 fans; **Danny called ≈8 p.m. on the kitchen phone; Gina held** ("Bring the rich one Friday. Tell him don't wear nothing nice."). **Gina's color

(🔒 never explained) · Danny: "She know about me?" per `e18_danny` · Skye knows Danny is in prison [gap candor] and never raised it on the call · what was typed at 2 a.m.
(`e18_text`) never spoken on the call · E19's first meeting knows the porch call happened · Skye's "Write that down… I'll remember it wrong on purpose."
**Continuity notes.** Sunset Youngstown ≈4:56 p.m.; Sun Nov 29 moonset ≈4:30 a.m. but overcast all night (s16b). Studio City is Eastern −3; New Orleans −1. Kickoff
1:00 p.m. ET, the game ends ≈4:00 — s21 is ≈4:05, departure ≈4:50. Gina's New Moon count: twice by Thu, third time Sat. Frank does **not** drink Thursday or Sunday
(thermos). Nonna sleeps in the front-room chair (since Nov 8); she never climbs the stairs this weekend. The seventh stair talks; there are fourteen. The side door has a
spring and usually bangs. The Madonna's carnations: red and white, cellophane, from the all-night gas station on Market Street (a ten-minute walk).

### S1E19 — "Nobody" (Mon Nov 30 – Sun Dec 6, 2009; tag Sun ≈9:15 a.m. PST, the 4th-floor lounge)
> Final revision, collapsed against `saves/after-s1e18.json` (wit 58, reckless 39, candor 68, sincerity 125, grace 34, restraint −16, honesty 35; Jack resolve 6, Lena 5).

- **Mon Nov 30.** s01 7:40 a.m.: Theo reads the News Feed aloud (Skye's likes; Jack's Feb 3 Note "25 Random Things About Me" — No. 4 Gina, No. 9 the Summa twice). Rosie's two
  crayon princes taped over Theo's desk; **Nonna's twenty folded in the frame of Theo's mirror**. s02 Sully's, MNF **Saints 38–17 Patriots (11–0)**; the brass bell; Julian rings
  it on the third TD; Kevin's napkin margin: WEIS — FIRED, TIGER — WD (Chevron, Thousand Oaks); Tiger's crash = hydrant then tree, 2:25 a.m. Nov 27 (no conduct invented for
  Elin). Browns lost to the Bengals 16–7 (Sun Nov 29). Skye's texts 7:02/7:03. Jack leaves at the two-minute warning (`e19_exit`); Kevin writes COSTIGAN — LEFT 4Q, 2:00.
  s03/s03a the launch-ramp lot 8:50–10:40 p.m.: the strapless "dress with opinions" (Burbank fitting); tuxedo jacket not hers; her **marimba alarm at 10:40** stops it; dropped at
  **the Marine Lab turnout** (her drop point from now on, never the Anacapa lot). Odyssey back glass: WHO DAT · 11–0. Last time in a lot together = Tue Nov 24 (E17).
- **Tue Dec 1.** s04 the Fishbowl, **Obama at West Point (8 p.m. ET = 5 p.m. PT)**; Yusuf: "You left the first time. My father watched you leave. If you're going to come,
  stay." **Tariq: IED, Helmand; alive; Landstuhl; to be flown home.** Lena tells Yusuf "I'll get Kevin" on the stairs [go] / sits a flight down [stay]; **Kevin texts Jack
  6:31 p.m. "Airport at 6 AM with Yusuf. First flight north."** [go]. Margot's prop is the **Moleskine** (legal pads belong to Laney's meeting and Debbie Callender).
- **Wed Dec 2.** s05 1:05–2:45 a.m. the wharf rail, **full moon overhead**; Skye on her mother's Studio City balcony (5 a.m. call); "Do the room" (her reads are general, Jack
  supplies the facts); Lena's text **1:48 a.m.** ("Up? I keep thinking about Yusuf's mother"). Jack walks home ≈3:40, sleeps ≈4 hrs. **PM1 sent 5:12 a.m. ("proof of five")**,
  opened 8:10 on the east-stair landing (best reception on four); Kevin back from the airport. s06 Hale: Yusuf has gone home, keep his chair; **final is oral, Wed Dec 16, pairs,
  20 min each, defend your opponent's best argument**. s06a Skye's magazine questionnaire (Valentine's issue; soap-snow set in Burbank, 71°). **The Tiger voicemail + website
  "transgressions" statement both Dec 2** (period sheet).
- **Thu Dec 3.** St. Francis Xavier; s07a 12:10 Mass; Mrs. Fitch's flyer: **Advent party Fri Dec 11, 7 p.m., Newman House, $10 white elephant, "FR. OSEI REQUESTS NO
  MISTLETOE."** Becca: still on the Christmas-card list; Daisy in the photo. s08 last PHIL 1 section (Kierkegaard; *Who answers for Isaac?*). s08a 9 p.m. Skye from the garage
  under her mother's building; *Jersey Shore* premiere (Laney/Theo texts 10:06–10:12).
- **Fri Dec 4.** s09 THE SITUATION ROOM (Fishbowl; orange bulbs; Cheetos in a punch bowl; spray tan unopened); Theo replays "take your name off your phone"; times Jack's pocket
  with a stopwatch, says no number. Skye 11:52 / 11:55 ("ohio").
- **Sat Dec 5.** s10 12:05 a.m. the Saab north (Rumours ejected; "Thunder Road" on a Santa Barbara classic-rock station); truck lives; Lordstown Cobalts; LA invite (network
  party, piano in the pool; Jack works Sat 7–1, the Hobart). s10a 1:35 ranch road west of the highway, cattle on warm asphalt, heifer tag 61, *Questions Only*. s11 1:50 turnout
  where the coast turns the corner (Jalama model; **≈40 miles from campus**; a working lighthouse down the coast); heat step (her in his lap; flannel/camisole off; his shirt
  open). **Alarm Five 3:10 a.m.** (27 days; Margot said 26) — Theo's texts 3:12–3:15 stop it. Drive back ≈40 min: **dropped 3:58, on the lawn 4:03**; horn long silenced,
  MFD walking every floor before anyone goes in; Priya ran roll twice; Theo's "shower" cover; Kevin's pencil question mark. Skye: "I don't take people home." s12a 11:40 a.m.
  Marine Lab turnout in daylight: **Skye starts at Marisol in January (spring, undeclared)**; math placement booklet; studio teacher Irene; proficiency test at 16; Cassidy sent
  to a Swiss school in the spring (off camera); fractions; Jack goes in a tide pool to the knee. Alabama 32–13 Florida (SEC title; ended ≈4:30 p.m. PT). s13 2:30 laundry room:
  Theo + Laney's Saturday 2:30 "study group" (alliance meeting 1; Pi Lam legal pad). s14/s14a confession 3:35–4:00 (penance: a decade for her, by name).
- **Sun Dec 6 (St. Nicholas).** s15 the Anchor 12:40–1:00 a.m.: Lena in Kevin's Odyssey (Julian in Oakland — brother **Daniel, Navy, two weeks' leave**); Gloria: "Pecan came to
  see if you were here." s16 Walgreens 1:35: **Maria fills 200 shoes at St. Brigid's every St. Nicholas Eve (did them Friday this year; Jack and Danny carried boxes)**; Lena:
  "Where do you go, Jack?" Reliquary **No. 10**. s17–s19v the breakwater/Mission St. 2:10–2:55 — **Lena's Greek final Tue Dec 8 (Book Nine at sight): "The languages go
  early"** (language finals run before finals week Dec 14–18; Hale's oral stays Wed Dec 16). s20 the shoe raid 3:20–4:10 (Priya's NO SHOES IN HALLWAYS; Uggs 217, Rafi's
  flip-flops 204, clementine at 305, Doug's Crocs 420 + Kevin's keys, **Theo's good cordovan loafers outside 412**, Priya's card at 401, headlamp 4:05). s21 laundry room 4:30–
  ≈5:00 (she steps back first). **PM2 sent 4:40 a.m.** (hotel-bathroom mirror at the network party). s22 Theo out barefoot ≈5:30, back 6:40 wet to the shin, carrying his loafers;
  **Jack invites Theo for Christmas himself ("Pop meant it in the garage… And I'm asking")**; Theo: "I have things. In LA." / "Tell your father I heard him." Theo: "I'm from
  La Cañada. We don't have saints. We have donors." (No denomination set.) s23 Margot's whiteboard: UNEXPLAINED CITRUS — FLOORS 2–4.

### S1E20 — "Stay" (Sun Dec 6 – Fri Dec 11, 2009; tag Fri ≈5:52 a.m. PST, the corner of Del Playa)
> Final revision, collapsed against `saves/after-s1e19.json` (wit 63, reckless 43, candor 73, sincerity 125, control 19, charm 26; grace 37, honesty 40, restraint −19; Jack resolve 4, Lena 4).
> No E19 gates remain; E19 played values are stated flatly (COSTIGAN — PRESENT; "This one isn't a Thursday"; "You never answered the Bible"; the `pic2` caption; turnout/cows/"sometime"/"your everybody"; Osei's "two in the morning, and a car").
> Live gates: `trait.wit>=56` (s18a [Riposte]), `trait.sincerity>=100` (s19a [Unguarded]), `resolve.jack<=3` (s17b narration). 36 scenes; ≈22.2–23.6k words per path (mean ≈22.9k); 12 keys and 1–5 whispers per path (8 in file);
> forks `e20_mass` (s09a noon / s09b dusk / s09c skip), `e20_song` (reckless skips s15a); duel `why` (s18–s18v, unofficial — no `note:record`).

- **Sun Dec 6 (Advent II).** s01 Sully's 10:05 a.m.: **Saints 33–30 Redskins in OT at FedExField, Landover MD (12–0)** — Meachem strips the interception back for a TD before the half; Suisham misses a short FG late;
  Brees–Meachem long to tie; OT: Sellers fumbles after a catch, long review, **Saints' ball on Washington's 37**, a handful of plays, **Hartley 18 yds at ≈1:25 p.m. PT**. The page never says who won the toss.
  **Chargers 30–23 Browns in Cleveland** (same 10 a.m. PT window; Browns 1–11) on the crawl. Lions lost to the Bengals 23–13 (Dale at the bar: "It had to be the Bengals"). Kevin's napkin: OT — SUDDEN DEATH (SUNDAY,
  ADVENT II) / COSTIGAN — PRESENT. Laney's Pi Lam semiformal (Sloane's date in a kilt; "Single Ladies" ×4); Laney's Ugg of clementines. **Julian left Oakland after lunch; heard the end in Gilroy at a Chevron; the cow
  (dry brown field) reached Lena on the kick.** s02 3:40 p.m.: Theo's gray cashmere throw over Jack ("holding it like a rosary"); Skye 1:34/1:40 ("tomorrow. im asking"). s03 6:10 p.m. east-stair call: Maria back from
  Trumbull; **Danny went to the prison library about Flannery O'Connor** (Maria read it off an envelope); **the November phone bill: 409 texts** ("Who is it?") per `e20_bill`; Lena's saint is in Nonna's missal (red).
  s04 the 9 p.m. Mass (Kenzie's capo; Joey lights the second candle), courtyard ≈9:55: Julian's brother **Daniel** home on Navy leave (a flag on the piano); **"Thursday. Bastille. I finished it. Will you stay to the end?"**
  Lena's resolve 5.
- **Mon Dec 7.** **First winter storm** (sideways rain off the Lagoon). s05: **Theo's black duffel on the closet shelf — denim jacket with a red Ferris-wheel FRESNO FAIR pin** (🔒 shown, never interpreted); the Burberry.
  Skye 7:41/7:42 ("told you id ask somewhere worse"). Hale's last Monday seminar 1:00–2:15. s06 2:35 p.m. turnout → 101 south in the rain (Gaviota, Refugio, El Capitan, Carpinteria flags at half-staff for Pearl
  Harbor, the Rincon over the riprap, the Conejo Grade); Skye's mother calls (the reunion picture; the publicist changed her favorite book back to *New Moon*); Gil the Ralphs driver; Joni Mitchell "California."
  s07 Stage 9, Burbank (5:40 p.m.): the camera test — a 1962 revival-tent scene, the preacher's daughter forgives the thief (Lonnie); **the white dress, 40 covered buttons, hem to chin**; visitor pass MY COUSIN FROM
  OHIO; rain tower over a painted lot (a Studebaker with taped chrome); her studio name Miss Callender; the guard. [`stood_in_rain`] Jack stood in for the thief's stand-in 9 minutes at ≈7:10. s07a the honeywagon
  8:15 (heat step: no buttons; she counts to "sixty-one"). s07b takes four–six (director: "More grace"); **take six is played dry — the kept take**. s07c: Lena 11:02 p.m. ("Book Nine is 566 lines… Pray for me at 8");
  wrap 12:40 a.m.; **on the drive home Jack tells Skye it's a holy day, the Immaculate Conception, and writes it on the back of her hand in Sharpie under the grip's nephew's email**; "praying" 1:40 a.m.; dropped at the
  turnout 3:40.
- **Tue Dec 8 (Immaculate Conception; holy day).** s08 3:55–3:58 a.m.: **Doug Pruitt sleepwalks — the alarm-puller** (Jack catches his wrist on the east-stair pull station; "the raid boss is in the elevator"); per
  `e20_doug`: Margot at 305 (CASE CLOSED on the whiteboard Wed) / Kevin (chair under the knob, then a Christmas bell on dental floss; health center after finals) / nobody. Jack in bed 4:15; alarm set for 11:40.
  Lena's Greek final at 8 (Book Nine at sight; *Outis*, 9.366; "I think I passed" 10:12). Kevin 10:30: Fitch's times 7, 12:10, 5:30. s09a 12:10 [noon]: Osei's homily on *kecharitōmenē* (perfect participle; "God gives
  first"; "In Kumasi a physician catches whatever arrives"; "You cannot earn a gift. You can open it."); Joey at the sign of peace; Osei: "On a Tuesday." / "I was there."; **Becca's white-elephant list (Fri 7 p.m., $10,
  nothing that eats); Thomas the hermit crab flies home to Idaho in a Tupperware at 33 ppt**. s09b 5:30 [dusk]: candles; Julian in a gray cardigan, has every response, doesn't receive, gets the *Ave* by ear the second
  time; the RAZR buzzes on the last *Ave* (PM3, 6:18). s09c [skip]: wakes 6:20 p.m. — **first holy day of obligation missed since sixteen**; Lena 6:41 (Fr. Osei's participle relayed); **Osei calls across Ocean Road from
  the steps: "God gives first. Even to the late."** The organist is still unnamed to Jack. s10 the Anchor: **Gloria gives Jack's Thursday to Hector (his girl due at Christmas)**; 9:20 the walk-in: **PM3 (rung 3)** —
  Skye's mother's Studio City bathroom (fish shower curtain), the white dress with all 40 undone, a strip of white cotton, a forearm across her chest, the skirt mostly shut but one bare thigh out of it, the phone up by
  her face; captions "you said you havent stopped. this is so you dont" / "wardrobe thinks its at the cleaners" / "all 40. tell me to do them back up. i DARE you" (6:18–6:20). Rudy opens the walk-in ("Mijo. Lettuce.").
- **Wed Dec 9.** s10a 1:10–1:45 a.m., the Greyhound bench / Skye in a Studio City 24-hr laundromat washing the dress (it lives); the dry take went to the network; her mother's one note: "*Tired.*" underlined and circled;
  she thinks the Immaculate Conception is the virgin birth ("Must be nice"). Skye shoots a cast picture on a Burbank dock at 10 a.m. s11 8:20: the case per `e20_doug` (Kevin redraws his question mark [control]).
  s12 Hale's last seminar: Yusuf's chair; Lena silent an hour ("Note the date"); **by Friday each emails Hale which of the other's arguments they'll defend; "If you choose a weak one in order to win, I shall know."**;
  Oslo tomorrow. **Julian played Lena the song Tue night in his garage.** s13/s13a the Brick, 8th floor, 11:20 p.m.–≈2:15 a.m.: **Lena picks Jack's "A man knows his own heart by what he says yes to."** Jack picks per
  `e20_best` (promise [`e20_best_weak`: Lena says he picked to win] / the dead / "you leave the light on" / "That's not hypocrisy. That's showing up"); phones side by side; Kevin at the next carrel ("That isn't studying").
  Lena: "It's good, Jack. It's really good." / "don't leave in the second chorus."
- **Thu Dec 10.** Lena emailed Hale at 7 a.m. (Latin subject line); Jack's is due Friday. s14 2:50 p.m. launch-ramp lot: **Skye took the math placement test** (science building, forty test-takers, an egg timer; "nobody
  looked at me"); result mailed in January; **the roll used "a name I don't use"** ("Ask me in a different daylight"); she's "up here tonight, at the house." s14a 4:30 the Fishbowl: **Obama's Nobel lecture** (Oslo;
  "Evil does exist in the world…"; "I face the world as it is"); Yusuf back last night — **Tariq did all eleven stairs Monday**; Margot's "who decides which villagers are the cost." s15 Bastille 9:10: **"Magdalena"** —
  Julian 7th on the board (a ukulele girl and Tanner first); Laney seats Jack in Julian's chair and holds Lena's hand; Theo at the counter; Margot reviews for the *Tide*. Lena resolve 4. Per `e20_song`: looked back /
  watched Julian / **left in the second chorus**. s15a [stayed] ≈9:40 ("You stayed. She wanted you here." [after sincerity]); Jack out before ten, "Out." s16 10:05 the Walgreens; calls Skye ("Eleven minutes").
  s16a: **Jack turns the RAZR off in her car (from now on, with Skye, the phone is off)**; "I've decided I want to [take people home]." **Skye's house: two-story shingle on Del Playa, porch that leans, empty surfboard rack
  ("the boys" in Baja), a darkroom (red bulb, towel, vinegar); "Sol's in LA."** She'll live there in January. s17 10:40 her room (Christmas lights half out, mattress under the window, clothes rack, jar of beach glass, the
  O'Connor with CON struck through, the white dress back from her trunk on the door hook); heat step (shirtless, lying down for the first time). s17b ≈11:20 **Jack's first no** (`e20_no`) — she never asked in words;
  "I said not in a *car*. This isn't a car."; "You can go. I'll drive you." He sits down against the clothes rack and stays.
- **Fri Dec 11.** s18–s18v 11:40 p.m.–≈1:40 a.m.: mint tea at 11:30; Cat Power "The Greatest" from her laptop; her questions (mortal sin; Catechism on knowledge and intent; confession; Song of Songs; "You'd make love to
  a notary"; nine weddings); duel `why`. s19 3:30: **"Do you think I'm a slut?"** — his no; the jealousy is his; the teaching is for judging himself (CCC 1861 paraphrase — justice and mercy); per `e20_dignity`.
  "You're the only person who's ever told me no and meant it." s19a 4:15: **"It's a big thing for me. And it's a no right now." — "I can work with that."**; Advent's rose candle Sunday; sunrise ≈6:50; confession
  ("He won't know your name… That's the deal." / "Good deal."); January: a bicycle, a backpack. s20 5:40 the porch (waning crescent, ≈27% lit, up in the southeast; "the boys come back Sunday"; "My cousin from Ohio").
  s20e 5:52: RAZR back on; **Lena's 11:41 p.m. text** (left: "You left in the second chorus." / stayed: "Thank you for staying." [+ "He said you told him."]); unanswered; first rain at the Grove. The Advent party is tonight 7 p.m.

  Nonna") marks his page. **PHIL 1 final, the Events Center floor, 8 a.m.: PROVE TO ME YOU HAVE A SOUL. YOU HAVE THREE HOURS AND I'VE HAD BREAKFAST.** Evan walks the aisles; Lena laughs into her sleeve; Jack writes Nonna's beads.
